<p> Ottawa Citizen - RSS Feedhttps : **28;24370;TOOLONG , 26 Feb 2020 12:12:00 +0000The Lumineers come to Ottawa to share moving story of addiction 's toll on a familyhttp : **34;24400;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;24436;TOOLONG ... title= " The Lumineers. " alt= " The Lumineers. " data-portal-copyright= " Danny Clinch " **28;24472;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1566850 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The **33;24502;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;With guests Mt . Joy and J.S. **30;24537;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;When &amp; where : 7 p.m . March 7 , Canadian Tire **30;24569;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Tickets : $60.50 and up , available at ticketmaster.ca and **46;24601;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On their third album , III , the Colorado-based folk rockers The Lumineers tell the story of a family torn apart by addiction . It&amp;rsquo ; s a concept album in that each of the 10 songs add to the story , and each one has a dramatic video , directed by Kevin Phillips , to go with it . In this interview , **27;24649;TOOLONG Wesley Schultz explains the origin of the ambitious project , and how he hopes the videos help people understand the story behind the songs . The band is on a world tour that stops in Toronto , Montreal and Ottawa in early March . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What inspired the story of the Sparks family ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : It came from what was going on in my own life . My wife and I have been taking care of this family member on her side . We tried to buy her a house and fix that up for her . She 's an alcoholic and we had to evict her from that because she was wrecking the house and herself , and we tried to put her in rehab and she escaped a number of times , did n't want to be there . She ended up in jail in another state , and is now living in and out of shelters and basically homeless . There 's been 10 years of that , and it led to trying to make sense of it , and not hate that person but maybe try to empathize a little bit and understand it and cope with it . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : You made it a fictional family . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : Yeah , I was trying to tell the story but also not hurt someone in the process . I 've been to Al-Anon meetings , and been around people who are addicts and I think the common thing is shame . I did n't want to shame this person with these stories but I wanted to tell the story as it was and not sugarcoat it , or make it a caricature that was n't real and did n't feel real . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Were you concerned the whole concept might not connect with fans ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : Yeah , we thought it was sort of a niche idea that might alienate a lot of people but it 's been really unifying for us . The amount of people that have said they can relate to something like this , that number is way bigger than I could have imagined . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;24678;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;24714;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : The videos are a really compelling part of the project . What inspired that idea ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : Something I really loved was watching the series of videos by Florence and the Machine a couple of albums ago . And then my wife showed me some videos from Lemonade , Beyonce 's project and I was equally blown away . But I also started to notice that there was a percentage of fans that never really understood some of the stories being told . At shows , I would sometimes say a little about the backstory , and through that , I realized that a lot of people did n't know what I was singing about . So trying to couple this with visuals appealed to me because I felt like I could further tell that story . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Was there a certain song that people misconstrued ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : I can give the example of the song , Ho Hey : It was about a breakup . But everybody started dancing to it at their weddings . I felt like I was in the twilight zone . But that 's also what 's so great about music , you can write something and someone can interpret it a different way . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : I guess people do n't always listen to lyrics . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : I 'm guilty of it , too . We 'll pick out one lyric or one line and that will define the whole song , and you ignore the story . I think that 's just the human thing . We just sort of skim things and cherry pick the things that speak to us . If anything , I 'd blame myself for failing to communicate well enough for people to know what I was singing about . The video idea was another way to communicate with an audience about what the songs were about to me. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24750;TOOLONG ... title= " The Lumineers. " alt= " The Lumineers. " data-portal-copyright= " Danny Clinch " **28;24786;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1566850 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Was it a surprise that the video series got into the Toronto International Film Festival ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : That was a really really big deal for us . I remember telling the director , Kevin Phillips , that we got in , and he did n't believe me . He made this beautiful movie , Super Dark Times , and that film had n't gotten in so I think he was really caught off guard . The idea was that we wanted to make something that might be mistaken for a film , and it was a really validating feeling to be seen by TIFF as a film . We got into one of the top 5 film festivals in the world with a movie with no dialogue . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Do you think fans are getting the story this time ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : I think it 's like this underground thing . There are a lot of people who know of the story . Some followed it closely and some are brand new to it . But i think the music itself is always the starting point , and I think that 's in a good place so it allows people to be curious . If the album was n't any good , the story would n't really matter . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : And how 's the live show going ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : So far , it 's been great . This is an arena run so it 's a totally different stage and setup . It 's been a big learning curve for us . It 's the biggest stage we 've played on compared to any other tour . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;It involves a catwalk that surrounds an enclosed pit of people , and outside that , more people are standing and seated people behind that , so there 's a lot of moving parts and places and the show does n't really stand still too often . So for us , it really flies by . You get up on stage and you 're taking a bow before you know it . This is the fun part for us. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; lsaxberg@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24816;TOOLONG ... mother laments the scourge of opioids and the daughter she could not save&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24851;TOOLONG ... of Ottawa 's modern franchise : What 's behind Ottawa 's changing food scene&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24886;TOOLONG ... bets , Feb. 27 to March 4 : Paul Dewar fundraising concert , and a unique Rush cover band show&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Lynn SaxbergWed , 26 Feb 2020 12:12:00 **45;24921;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicLocal choir group sounds alarm over ' gouging ' resale ticketshttp : **33;24968;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;25003;TOOLONG ... title= " Ottawa 's Tone Cluster choir " alt= " Ottawa 's Tone Cluster choir " data-portal-copyright= " Mike Thompson " **28;25039;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1565140 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;25069;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; The producers of a local queer choir are urging their supporters to purchase tickets to an upcoming Meridian Theatres fundraiser directly from the venue after a swath of seats surfaced on various online ticket resellers this week . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Organizers of the local Tone Cluster choir have been raising funds for the group since 2005 , with this year&amp;rsquo ; s marquee event &amp;mdash ; the Show Tunes Showdown on April 18 on the main stage of Meridian Theatres at Centrepointe &amp;mdash ; raising money for the group to travel to a gala LGBTQ choral festival in Minneapolis . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But while tickets , currently being sold at a face value of $34.75 , are still available through the authorized box office , Tone Cluster organizers started sounding the alarm after noticing tickets being resold online at grossly inflated prices . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some resellers are offering their tickets starting at $67 , or roughly double face value , while others are demanding more than $350 for a single seat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We don&amp;rsquo ; t know whether these are fake tickets , or whether these resellers have bought a block of ( legitimate ) tickets and are just selling them at higher prices , &amp;rdquo ; said choir publicist Heather Daley. &amp;ldquo ; But it&amp;rsquo ; s really strange how high some of the prices are&amp;hellip ; some of these places are selling tickets for over $400. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Organizers still don&amp;rsquo ; t know how many tickets have been sold through unauthorized vendors , raising concerns that some tickets may not be honoured once the date of the show arrives . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We don&amp;rsquo ; t know if anybody has been buying them ( through resellers ) and we&amp;rsquo ; re asking people to only buy them through Meridian &amp;mdash ; they are the only authorized ticket agent , &amp;rdquo ; Daley said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The ( resold ) tickets may not be honoured when they come to the show because they might be fake tickets . But we don&amp;rsquo ; t know . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Daley said she found it especially odd that the resale market appears to be flooded with inflated tickets for an event that has yet to sell out , with many seats still available at the regular price through the authorized vendor . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Aside from concerns their audience members are being &amp;ldquo ; gouged&amp;rdquo ; in the resale market , the choir is also concerned about the prospect of raising enough funds through authorized ticket sales to finance a trip to an international gala choral festival in the U.S. in July . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Show Tune Showdown is our biggest fundraiser of the year , and this gala event in July attracts over 7,000 choristers from across the world , &amp;ldquo ; said Daley. &amp;ldquo ; Tone Cluster has done really well there in the past when we&amp;rsquo ; ve gone to perform , and it&amp;rsquo ; s given us a really fabulous name internationally . But we need to fundraise to get there . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; More information on the upcoming show is available at &lt;a href= " http : //tonecluster.ca " &gt; tonecluster.ca&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;25105;TOOLONG " **35;25135;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Twitter.com/helmera &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN ARTS AND LIFE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;25172;TOOLONG ... bets , Feb 20-26 : A Mardi Gras bash and a Mary Poppins sing-along at the NAC&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;25208;TOOLONG ... Here 's who 's coming to Bluesfest ! &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Aedan HelmerFri , 21 Feb 2020 21:31:57 **45;25278;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMusicNewsAlanis ! Blink-182 ! Travis Tritt ! Nelly ! Here 's who 's coming to Bluesfest ! http : **30;25325;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;25357;TOOLONG ... title= " Alanis Morissette performs at the New Orleans Jazz and Heritage Festival on Thursday , April 25 , 2019 , in New Orleans . " alt= " Alanis Morissette performs at the New Orleans Jazz and Heritage Festival on Thursday , April 25 , 2019 , in New Orleans . " data-portal-copyright= " Amy Harris " **28;25393;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1562116 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Alanis Morissette , one of Ottawa 's most successful pop-music exports , will return to the nation 's capital this summer to perform a headlining show at RBC Bluesfest on July 12 , her first hometown concert in more than a decade . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The Ottawa-born superstar is a highlight of the Bluesfest program announced Tuesday , a typically wide-ranging and eclectic roster that also includes pop-rockers Blink-182 , country star Travis Tritt , R&amp;B singer Daniel Caesar , English prog heroes King Crimson , alt-rockers The National , perennial favourites Blue Rodeo , emo icons Dashboard Confessional , Indigenous tenor Jeremy Dutcher , rapper Nelly and many more . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25423;TOOLONG ... title= " Jim Cuddy of Blue Rodeo performs at the RBC Bluesfest on the grounds of the Canadian War Museum . " alt= " Jim Cuddy of Blue Rodeo performs at the RBC Bluesfest on the grounds of the Canadian War Museum . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;25459;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1562200 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;They join previously announced acts Rage Against The Machine , Jack Johnson , Marshmello and the Saints and Sinners tour , featuring Can-rockers Big Wreck , Headstones , Moist and The Tea Party , at this year 's edition of the festival , which runs from July 9-19 at LeBreton Flats Park on the grounds of the Canadian War Museum . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " We 've been trying to get this deal done for a few years , " said Bluesfest executive director Mark Monahan on booking Morissette . " We 've been very close the last couple of years , but of course , last year , she had a kid . This year , with her Broadway show and the new record , the timing was good . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25489;TOOLONG ... title= " Fans at the RBC Ottawa Bluesfest , July 10 , 2018. " alt= " Fans at the RBC Ottawa Bluesfest , July 10 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;25525;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1229593 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Morissette 's third child with her husband , &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;rapper &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Mario " Souleye " Treadway&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; , was born last August , and she 's been plotting her comeback ever since . Her first new album in eight years , Such Pretty Forks in the Road , is expected for release on May 1 , and she 's embarking on a tour to celebrate the 25th anniversary of her multimillion-selling landmark Jagged Little Pill , which is also the inspiration for a new musical currently running on Broadway . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;About 100 acts are so far confirmed for the nine-day festival , which will feature performances on three outdoor stages ( City , Vid ? otron and Bluesville ) and the Barney Danson Theatre inside the Canadian War Museum . Of the $20-million budget , roughly half is earmarked for talent , an increase of about 15 per cent over last year , Monahan said . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25555;TOOLONG ... title= " US rapper Nelly performs on stage during a male-only concert at King Abdullah Economic City on December 14 , 2017. " alt= " US rapper Nelly performs on stage during a male-only concert at King Abdullah Economic City on December 14 , 2017. " data-portal-copyright= " ALI AL-NAIMI " **28;25591;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1562201 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Pre-Christmas sales of early-bird passes are also up , he added , and there 's been a barrage of inquiries about last week 's Rage Against the Machine announcement . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " It 's arguably the biggest thing we 've ever done , " Monahan said . " The band went on sale with 40 dates last week and sold out . The response is huge . We also anticipate a lot of people coming from out of town . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Not to be lost in the shuffle is the blues , the festival 's namesake genre . The popular blues jams in the Danson theatre will return , with the Tony D Band hosting the first weekend and The Split on the second weekend . Other blues-based acts headed to Bluesfest include sax phenom Vanessa Collier , Memphis soul outfit Southern Avenue , Australian guitarist Lloyd Spiegel , Seattle soul revivalists The Dip , resident brass section The Texas Horns , and Dwayne Dopsie and the Zydeco Hellraisers. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Dozens of Ottawa-area acts will be showcased , too , including Alanna Sterling , Amanda Lowe , Angelique Francis , Kimberly Sunstrum , Sly High , Lyle Odjick and The Northern Steam and Murray Kinsley and Wicked Grin , to name a few . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25621;TOOLONG ... title= " Three-year-old Nathaniel Hayes was dancing with his dad at Bluesfest Saturday July 6 , 2019 . It is Hayes third year taking in the festival . " alt= " Three-year-old Nathaniel Hayes was dancing with his dad at Bluesfest Saturday July 6 , 2019 . It is Hayes third year taking in the festival . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;25657;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1424654 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The festival , which attracts tens of thousands of people each day , will be a major test for the city 's new LRT system as the nightly exodus sends everyone into the streets at the same time . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " We 're hoping the LRT will make it easier to come on and off site , but we understand we will be supplementing it with some extra buses , " Monahan said . " We 're just starting to plan that out , but hopefully it 's a positive situation . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A one-day presale of full-festival passes starts at 10 a.m . Feb. 19 and runs until midnight that day . Adult passes , youth passes ( under 19 ) , young-adult passes ( under 25 ) and VIP Club passes are available . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Day tickets , pick-three and pick-five packages , and the Metropolitan VIP passes go on sale at 10 a.m . Feb. 20 . Go to ottawabluesfest.ca for complete details and pricing . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25687;TOOLONG ... title= " Travis Tritt performs . " data-portal-copyright= " Ian Kucerak " **28;25723;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1562199 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Meanwhile , here 's a preliminary , day-by-day look at the 2020 edition of Bluesfest : &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 9&lt;/strong&gt; : Atlanta rapper 6LACK , trans singer Ezra Furman , Polaris champ Jeremy Dutcher , Toronto 's Charlotte Day Wilson , singer-songwriter Leif Vollebeck and reggae artist Protoje . An additional headliner is expected to be confirmed in the coming weeks . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 10&lt;/strong&gt; : Daniel Caesar steps up to the headlining slot , with other performances by Toronto 's Ali Gatie , New Pornographers and Talking Heads co-founder Jerry Harrison and former touring guitarist Adrian Belew , who will be joined by Turkuaz to mark the 40th anniversary of the Heads ' album , Remain in Light . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 11&lt;/strong&gt; : Australian pop star Dean Lewis , the Be Alright singer , closes out the first Saturday night , with earlier appearances by King Crimson , indie rockers July Talk , bluesy Brit rocker Barns Courtney and more . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25753;TOOLONG ... title= " Alanis Morissette performs at the New Orleans Jazz and Heritage Festival on Thursday , April 25 , 2019 , in New Orleans . " alt= " Alanis Morissette performs at the New Orleans Jazz and Heritage Festival on Thursday , April 25 , 2019 , in New Orleans . " data-portal-copyright= " Amy Harris " **28;25789;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1562116 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 12&lt;/strong&gt; : Morissette comes home , accompanied by two of her tour openers , Garbage and Joan Osborne , along with separate shows by Dashboard Confessional , The Cinematic Orchestra and many more . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;25819;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 14&lt;/strong&gt; : The festival takes a break on July 13 before resuming with a main-stage lineup that includes Jack Johnson , Vance Joy and Jade Bird , as well as Montreal 's Patrick Watson and Sharon Van Etten on other stages . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 15&lt;/strong&gt; : It 's pop-punk night with Blink-182 , Billy Talent and Grandson , plus the psychedelic multi-instrumentalism of Australia 's Tash Sultana. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 16&lt;/strong&gt; : Digital mega-star Marshmello is on the main stage , with rapper Lil Tecca , indie folk-rocker Cat Power and DJ Shadow also performing . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 17&lt;/strong&gt; : Rage Against The Machine storms the main stage . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;Jul ... 18&lt;/strong&gt; : The final Saturday is a hip hop-throwback night , with Boyz II Men , Nelly , Deltron 3030 and Arrested Development on the main stage , while the Saints and Sinners tour rocks the Vid ? otron stage . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;July 19 : The festival wraps up with a main-stage nod to country music , featuring Blue Rodeo , Travis Tritt and Jessica Mitchell . ArtsMusicConcert preview : Theory of a Deadman , playing Ottawa on Monday , speak out on Say Nothing tourhttp : **36;26148;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;26186;TOOLONG ... title= " Theory of a Deadman . " alt= " Theory of a Deadman . " data-portal-copyright= " Christian Lantry " **28;26222;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1562419 " /&gt; **30;26252;TOOLONG of a Deadman Say Nothing World Tour **30;26284;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 24 , 7 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Bronson Centre , 211 Bronson Ave . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //ticketmaster.ca " &gt;ticketmaster ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Strangers is the second single from Theory of a Deadman&amp;rsquo ; s seventh album Say Nothing . The new song addresses the dystopian state of social and political affairs in America today , mentioning such realities as Kids afraid of being shot up/Shooter drills are how they brought up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lead singer/songwriter Tyler Connolly calls from Los Angeles on Nov. 14 , 2019 , to talk about the sharply focused tune . At the same time as the interview takes place , Saugus High School in Santa Clarita , Calif. , is under fire as a student with a gun shoots five schoolmates , killing two , before killing himself . By the end of the week there will be two other mass shootings leaving the death toll at 10. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Par for the course , Connolly says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Strangers was the very first song written for the new album , and it clearly set the tone that the rest of it was going to take being about politics , gun violence and polarization , &amp;rdquo ; said Connolly . &amp;ldquo ; The worst we had growing up in Vancouver was earthquake drills , so this is something I don&amp;rsquo ; t even understand that these kids accept as normal . I feel like I grew up in a completely different time and it&amp;rsquo ; s not getting better ; it&amp;rsquo ; s scary and it&amp;rsquo ; s bad . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Speaking to America&amp;rsquo ; s political divisiveness might not seem standard subject matter for the hard-rocking Theory of a Deadman &amp;mdash ; ironically now known as Theory , as the &amp;ldquo ; Deadman&amp;rdquo ; in the name was upsetting to some sensitive sorts in its U.S. fan base . But the single Rx ( Medicate ) from 2017&amp;rsquo ; s Wake Up Call was platinum certified and it carried a clear message . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rx ( Medicate ) was about the prescription-drug abuse epidemic sweeping North America and produced a hit video as well . The band partnered up with Shatterproof , a non-profit group focused on **27;26316;TOOLONG education initiatives , to spread the message . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; History of Violence , the leadoff single from the new record , directly tackles domestic abuse and advocates fans contacting the National Domestic Violence Hotline in the event that they , or someone they know , is dealing with abuse . Having built its reputation on pretty straightforward hard rock , this new direction in lyrical content is matched by a progressing pop-rock sound . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Connolly says he&amp;rsquo ; s cool with the transition to more topical subject matter in songs . It&amp;rsquo ; s where he is in his life . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s different , but I find it very easy to write more from my opinion and perspective , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Of course , I am very careful with that , as our fan base can be left or right wing and I don&amp;rsquo ; t want to be saying what&amp;rsquo ; s correct or not to anybody . What I&amp;rsquo ; m trying to do is be in the middle and say , &amp;lsquo ; Why don&amp;rsquo ; t we look at all of this together . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are 10 tunes on the new album . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among them is Affluenza , influenced by the Texas court case of Ethan Couch in which his attorney argued that the privileged white teen shouldn&amp;rsquo ; t be charged with four counts of intoxication manslaughter because his &amp;ldquo ; affluenza&amp;rdquo ; led him to not having the same moral boundaries as non-rich people . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26345;TOOLONG ... title= " Theory of a Deadman . " alt= " Theory of a Deadman . " data-portal-copyright= " Christian Lantry " **28;26381;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1562419 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; White Boy is inspired by the first-degree murder of 32-year-old Heather Heyer by a neo-Nazi domestic terrorist in Charlottesville , Va. , on Aug. 12 , 2017 . The song begins with a chilling sample of one of the news reports coming out of the city on that day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Say Nothing says things . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In a sense , it&amp;rsquo ; s kind of an ironic title , &amp;rdquo ; said Connolly . &amp;ldquo ; But isn&amp;rsquo ; t that how everyone is nowadays ? They want everyone to shut up , but they also have too much to say and want all to listen . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Connolly hopes that he , David Brenner ( guitar/backing vocals ) , Dean Back ( bass ) and Joey Dandeneau ( drums ) can engage audiences on the coming tour with a mix of classics from the back catalogue and a healthy dose of the new material . Say Nothing is very much a product of the band once again working with producer Martin Terefe ( Jason Mraz , Yungblud ) to achieve the anthemic pop that it wanted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; How does Connolly see the group recreating the grand , orchestral hooks of songs such as the opening track Black Hole in Your Heart live ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I do play piano live quite bit more now , but for the full deal we will use tracks , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Everybody does it now , and it sounds like it should be there , so we&amp;rsquo ; ll go with that . That song was really largely Martin&amp;rsquo ; s idea and , wow , it totally turned out fantastically as did the rest of the record . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With the exception of History of Violence , none of Say Nothing has been proven on stage . Connolly looks forward to delivering the new songs in concert . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; History of Violence is great to play ; some nights you get a little emotional thinking of the woman in the story , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s fun doing older songs such as Bad Girlfriend and stuff , but getting onstage and telling this really sad story is powerful and I expect the other new songs will be as well . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Connolly says ending Say Nothing on a positive note was essential to balance out the album&amp;rsquo ; s gloomier topics . It&amp;rsquo ; s All Good closes the record with a message that the singer says reflects his final outlook on life . The acoustic-guitar-driven song also suggests that Theory might have a new country record waiting in the wings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN ARTS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26411;TOOLONG ... ca n't sing ... I 've got to go ' an emotional Elton John cuts N.Z. concert short and postpones last two shows&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26446;TOOLONG ... Out : Bukhari , one of Ottawa 's few Yemeni restaurants , serves appealing , affordable dishes&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26481;TOOLONG ... Blink-182 ! Travis Tritt ! Nelly ! Here 's who 's coming to Bluesfest ! &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynTue , 18 Feb 2020 16:17:35 **45;26516;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicNew album by Canada-New Zealand artist Tami Neilsen designed for ' sustainable ' touringhttp : **33;26563;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;26598;TOOLONG ... title= " Tami Neilson , along with her brother Jay , are Canadian musicians who used to travel with their family band , fell in love with a New Zealander and moved there , where she started her solo career . Her style is torchy , rockabilly-influenced alt-country. " alt= " Tami Neilson , along with her brother Jay , are Canadian musicians who used to travel with their family band , fell in love with a New Zealander and moved there , where she started her solo career . Her style is torchy , rockabilly-influenced alt-country. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;26634;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1557926 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;26664;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Tami Neilson is no stranger to the road . The singer-songwriter with the big hair , an urgent twang in her soulful voice and a retro-rockabilly vibe has been gigging since she was a kid , cutting her teeth by criss-crossing Canada with her mom , dad and two brothers in their family band , The Neilsons. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;At the time , it seemed like no big deal for Neilson , who 's now 42 and well into her solo career , but she 's since realized that playing country music was not the experience of most Canadian teens . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " People always go , ' Wow , what a great way to grow up , ' " she said in a recent interview . " But it seemed normal . It 's not until you become an adult and look back and realize it was really not normal . It was an unusual way to grow up but also really special and unique . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;One of the advantages of it , she sees now , was that she never had to convince her parents of the viability of a career in the arts . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " A lot of artists talk about their upbringing like it was a real struggle having to prove to their family they can do it , and the family says , ' You should get a real job to fall back on . ' I never had that , " Neilson says . " I was raised by a couple of crazy musicians and it was always very much about nurturing creativity . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Even when she fell in love with a New Zealander and moved there to raise a family , her dream of being a professional musician never wavered . But it did require some adjustments as she navigated a music industry where no one knew her ( or her parents ) and country music was seen as old and boring . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Neilson has worked hard to change that mindset , winning a slew of New Zealand music awards along the way . It 's no wonder -- she 's a dynamite performer with an arsenal of lively foot-stompers , plus some heartbreaking , gospel-infused ballads , as demonstrated during a thrilling show at the Black Sheep Inn a couple of months ago . She also wowed audiences in New Orleans earlier this month as part of the Folk Alliance International conference and showcase festival . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Now she 's gearing up to release her seventh solo outing , CHICKABOOM ! , a sweet , feisty zinger of a record that 's already earning rave reviews ahead of its Feb. 14 release date . As she revealed in our interview , it marks a significant shift in her approach to the business of music . Here 's more of the discussion ( edited for length ) : &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;26700;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;26735;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What 's the mission with CHICKABOOM ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : It would be romantic to say it was born out of creativity but I 've been touring extensively for the past three years , internationally , and it really boiled down to , ' How can I make music that 's going to be sustainable ? ' The last few albums I 've been flying my entire band over from New Zealand for international tours , and when you talk about four people from New Zealand , that 's $10,000 before you even sing a note . That 's just the flights . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Was there a turning point ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : About a year ago I had an extensive European tour , with 20 shows in 22 days and no days off , with the full band from New Zealand . I lost my voice and I hit the wall . When I got home , I said something needs to change because it 's not sustainable financially or emotionally . The template of touring was created for young , single males , and I 'm none of those things . I 'm a 42-year-old mother of two who lives at the bottom of the world . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How did you reinvent things to suit your life ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : I decided to strip everything back , and write songs that can be performed by three people . They 're still punchy little firecrackers but they showcase the voice and the essence of the music . And then another really important part was having my brother , Jay , involved . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How so ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : He and I started writing songs specifically for the two of us and a drummer to perform , and I asked him to come touring again . He lives in Ontario , and has a settled routine at home so it was a big ask . But he got suckered in , and it makes such a big difference just having someone who 's part of your village on the road with you . We 've been trying out all the songs on unsuspecting audiences , and we 've had a really great response . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : It sounds like a new chapter for you . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : Yeah , it really is and hopefully it 's something that can be sustainable for this time in my life as a touring mom . It 's made such a huge difference to say to my boys , ' See you next week . ' Even though I 'm constantly jetlagged and away for a week every month , I 'm home for three weeks every month . It 's definitely a much better work-family balance doing it this way . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What are your hopes and dreams ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A : I hope the record connects with a lot of people and builds an even larger fan base . I think I 've come to the place of being able to create music and still have a thriving family life . That 's living the dream . And hey , I saw Wanda Jackson ( the 82-year-old Queen of Rockabilly ) come out with a walker decorated with fringe and rhinestones . That 's going to be me at 80 , hobbling out with my fringe and rhinestones. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;26771;TOOLONG " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " **49;26802;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN ARTS AND LIFE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26853;TOOLONG ... bets , Feb. 13 to 19 : Sandy Sharkey 's photos , and cellist Rachel Mercer&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26888;TOOLONG ... Against the Machine coming to Ottawa Bluesfest&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26923;TOOLONG ... preview : Revisiting the enduring influence of guitarist Lenny Breau&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Lynn SaxbergWed , 12 Feb 2020 17:04:08 **45;26958;TOOLONG ... " data-portal-copyright= " LISA O'CONNOR " **28;27081;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1548753 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Happy Valentine&amp;rsquo ; s Day , Beliebers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s when tickets go on sale for a Sept. 1 stop of Justin Bieber&amp;rsquo ; s Changes Tour at Canadian Tire Centre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ticket sales start at noon Feb. 14. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The international superstar from Stratford , Ont. picked Christmas Eve to release a video announcing a massive summer tour in support of a new album . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He&amp;rsquo ; ll also hit Montreal , Quebec City and Toronto . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bieber last performed in Ottawa in 2016. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;blockquote class= " twitter-tweet " data-width= " 550 " data-dnt= " true " &gt;&lt;p lang= " en " dir= " ltr " &gt;JUST ANNOUNCED : &lt;a href= " https : **26;27111;TOOLONG ? refsr ... @justinbieber&lt;/a&gt; brings the Changes Tour to &lt;a href= " https : **33;27139;TOOLONG ... at &lt;a href= " https : //twitter.com/CdnTireCtr ? refsrc= ... @CdnTireCtr&lt;/a&gt; on Tuesday , Sept. 1 . **27;27174;TOOLONG are available on Friday , Feb. 14 , at Noon . &lt;a href= " https : //t.co/lqRPRxYgzM " &gt;pic.twitter ... -- Canadian Tire Centre ( @CdnTireCtr ) &lt;a href= " https : **33;27203;TOOLONG ... 28 , **33;27238;TOOLONG &lt;script async src= " https : **33;27273;TOOLONG " charset= " utf-8 " &gt;&lt;/script&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;27308;TOOLONG ... data flag crisis of confidence in National Film Board senior management&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;27343;TOOLONG ... audience tastes are dictating the future of art in Ottawa&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;27378;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicLaila Biali primed to make festive music with the Central Band of the Canadian Armed Forceshttp : **34;27460;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;27496;TOOLONG ... title= " Toronto **25;27532;TOOLONG Laila Biali , who plays Dec. 10/11 , 2019 at Meridian Theatres at Centrepointe with the Central Band of the Canadian Armed Forces . " alt= " Toronto **25;27559;TOOLONG Laila Biali , who plays Dec. 10/11 , 2019 at Meridian Theatres at Centrepointe with the Central Band of the Canadian Armed Forces . " data-portal-copyright= " Rachel Cicoria " **28;27586;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1518108 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Toronto **25;27616;TOOLONG Laila Biali frequently visits Ottawa , usually with her trio to perform her music that effervescently blends jazz and pop . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But next week , Biali , whose eponymous album won this year&amp;rsquo ; s Juno Award for best vocal jazz album , will be heard in Ottawa in a different light . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Tuesday and Wednesday , she will be the featured guest when the Ottawa-based Central Band of the Canadian Armed Forces plays two holiday-themed concerts at Meridian Theatres at Centrepointe. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below , Biali , a 39-year-old who has toured with Sting , Suzanne Vega and Chris Botti , discusses the thrill of working with her new military collaborators , what has kept her busy in 2019 and what 2020 holds for her . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How would you compare guesting with the Forces band with leading your own band ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I rarely have the opportunity to perform with a large ensemble . When I started in jazz as a student at Humber College , my heroes were Kenny Wheeler and Maria Schneider . I dreamed of writing for a big band and even took a crack at it twice . Logistically , keeping a band that size together was a feat that reached beyond my patience and capacity , so I decided to return to , and stick with , small configurations. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But my love of the large ensemble sound is still potent , which makes it extra special to be this year&amp;rsquo ; s guest with the Central Band of the Canadian Armed Forces , in Ottawa , during the holidays . That checks a lot of bucket-list boxes for me , personally . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What will be on the program for next week 's concerts ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Capt . John Fullerton has put together a beautiful and varied set of four festive songs I will join the band on , plus a fun holiday sing-along at the end , in English and French . I will also have my own set , which will feature a solo song , and three songs performed with the CBCAF&amp;rsquo ; s rhythm section . And , of course , there will be additional songs performed by the CBCAF on their own . I really look forward to it ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How much preparation at home and rehearsing will you be able to do ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I have the charts and MP3s in hand , and I will join the band for rehearsals the day of show , next Tuesday . This is not at all uncommon in jazz , and it&amp;rsquo ; s partly what makes the music so thrilling . There will be some adrenaline involved for sure , but I know we&amp;rsquo ; ll all be polished and ready . The CBCAF has been rehearsing this material on its own , and I have the feeling I&amp;rsquo ; ll be walking into a turn-key musical situation . Just add water ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : You 'll soon be releasing your version of Joni Mitchell 's River . Why did you cover it ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Joni is one of my favourite artists , possibly the favourite . Last year , after she turned 75 , everyone jumped on the Joni bandwagon , which I did as well . Several arrangements resulted and it felt right to do a proper studio version of River , which had become a fan favourite in live performance . It&amp;rsquo ; s a song I love because it speaks to how the holidays can be difficult for some . It&amp;rsquo ; s poignant and heartbreaking , and feels like winter . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;27643;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;27679;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : You spent much of this fall touring in Europe . How did you crack into that market ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I&amp;rsquo ; ve loved touring Europe . I have an agent over there , Catherine Mayer with Just Jazz . Her father was the first European promoter to bring Ella Fitzgerald to their continent . Imagine that ! Catherine and her colleague , Andy Lindsley , work hard to connect me with opportunities across the pond , and they work closely with my German label , ACT Music , to build my presence in that market . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Are there differences between European listeners and the Canadian crowds who saw you this summer ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Canadian crowds are an enthusiastic bunch , delightful to play for . Europeans can be a bit more reserved , but equally appreciative . They clap long rather than loud , if that makes sense . Both are wonderful in different ways . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;27715;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;27751;TOOLONG &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;27787;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;27823;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What did it mean for you to win the Juno Award this year for Best Vocal Album ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : As you know , I&amp;rsquo ; ve been toiling away in this &amp;ldquo ; middle space&amp;rdquo ; between jazz and something other ( pop , folk , soul , singer/songwriter ) for years . On the eponymous release , I felt the various musical worlds I inhabited were finally and fully brought together . It felt like &amp;ldquo ; me , &amp;rdquo ; but I didn&amp;rsquo ; t expect the jazz community to hop on board as they did . The honour and recognition of a Juno was such a surprise ! I hadn&amp;rsquo ; t even written a proper thank you until I was sitting at my table the night of the awards and my friend , Jodi Proznick , encouraged me to jot something down . What a rush ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : After these Ottawa concerts , will you have some down time ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Actually , I won&amp;rsquo ; t get much time off . We just put the finishing touches on the master for my new album , Out of Dust . We start rolling out singles in January , and the full album will release in March , so there is a lot to accomplish . But I do intend to take a few days off &amp;mdash ; to celebrate 10 years with my partner in all things , Ben Wittman , with a quick trip down to Brooklyn , to spend time with his mother-in-law for Christmas and New Year&amp;rsquo ; s , and then a mini ski vacation in Vermont where hubby and son will ski and I will spa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What are you looking forward to in 2020 ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I&amp;rsquo ; m excited to share my new work . Stylistically , it&amp;rsquo ; s in a similar zone as the last release , but the songs take on the theme of overcoming &amp;mdash ; facing loss and challenges of various kinds head on . Death , politics , the environment , addiction . But it&amp;rsquo ; s all done in a hopeful and , when appropriate , even playful way . We have lots of tour dates on the books already , so you&amp;rsquo ; ll be hearing from me again before too long . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Holidays with the Central Band&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Featuring Laila Biali&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Dec. 10 and 11 , 7 p.m. &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Meridian Theatres at Centrepointe &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; $20 , $15 for defence community members ( with promo code ) at &lt;a href= " http : **26;27859;TOOLONG " &gt;mer ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;27887;TOOLONG " **32;27914;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN ARTS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;27948;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicMarshmello , Jack Johnson coming to Bluesfest 2020http : **36;28101;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;28139;TOOLONG ... title= " Marshmello is coming to Bluesfest. " data-portal-copyright= " HANDOUT " **28;28175;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1516273 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bluesfest fans are in for a holiday treat with the early announcement of two of the 10 headliners expected at next summer&amp;rsquo ; s festival , plus an earlybird ticket special . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Enigmatic DJ/producer Marshmello and singer-songwriter-surfer Jack Johnson are the first major acts confirmed for the 2020 edition of RBC Bluesfest . For Johnson , it&amp;rsquo ; s been three years since his Ottawa debut at CityFolk ; he will headline Bluesfest on July 14 , topping a bill that also includes Riptide singer Vance Joy and fast-rising British newcomer Jade Bird . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28205;TOOLONG ... title= " Marshmello is coming to Bluesfest. " alt= " Marshmello is coming to Bluesfest. " data-portal-copyright= " HANDOUT " **28;28241;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1516273 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , digital mega-star Marshmello , the EDM producer who wears a custom-made marshmallow helmet and was the first artist to perform a live , in-game concert on a virtual Fortnite stage this year , performs in real time on July 16 , headlining a youth-oriented program that also features 17-year-old Queens rapper Lil Tecca. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s the second year in a row that Bluesfest has announced acts and a ticket deal before Christmas , a strategy that not only reflects the festival&amp;rsquo ; s race to sign current artists but also a desire to benefit from the gift-giving season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28271;TOOLONG ... title= " Jack Johnson . " alt= " Jack Johnson . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28307;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1516272 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We had a pretty significant uptick in ticket sales last year , &amp;rdquo ; said Bluesfest director Mark Monahan. &amp;ldquo ; Although you never know exactly why people buy tickets , I think people like the idea of giving it as a holiday gift . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Earlybird pricing is available on regular and VIP day tickets for July 14 and 16 , as well as regular , Clubhouse and youth full-festival passes . Prices are the same as last year , with a regular adult full-festival pass ringing in at $205 . The online-only sale runs from 10 a.m . Tuesday to midnight Dec. 24 at ottawabluesfest.ca. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bluesfest organizers are also pledging to match charitable donations in recognition of Giving Tuesday , a global movement to support charities and non-profit organizations . Funds donated to Bluesfest&amp;rsquo ; s music education programs on Dec. 3 will be matched by the festival , whether or not a ticket is purchased . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Next year&amp;rsquo ; s instalment of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s biggest summer music festival runs from July 9-19 at LeBreton Flats Park on the grounds of the Canadian War Museum . Some 300,000 people attend the event every year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The full lineup is expected in mid-to-late February . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; lsaxberg@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28337;TOOLONG ... is a story of abuse ' : BBC airs interview with Virginia Giuffre , who says she was forced to have sex with Prince Andrew&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28372;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMusicNew Souljazz Orchestra song inspired by Abdirahman Abdi deathhttp : **35;28489;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;28526;TOOLONG ... title= " Pierre Chretien , bandleader of Ottawa 's Souljazz Orchestra , October 25 , 2019. " alt= " Pierre Chretien , bandleader of Ottawa 's Souljazz Orchestra , October 25 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;28562;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1493802 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Souljazz Orchestra&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;8:30 p.m . Nov. 1-2 , Black Sheep Inn , Wakefield&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Both shows are sold out . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Pierre Chr ? tien was returning home one summer day three years ago when he saw a commotion in the neighbourhood . His Hintonburg street was swarming with ambulances and police cruisers . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Watching the news on television , the Ottawa musician realized he had just missed witnessing police officers take down his Somali-Canadian neighbour , Abdirahman Abdi , who died during the incident . One of the officers was later charged with manslaughter . He has pleaded not guilty . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The experience provoked Chr ? tien enough to sit down and write a song , now released on Souljazz Orchestra 's scorching ninth album , Chaos Theories . The fiery tune , Police The Police , stings with lightning-quick jabs of rhythmic , funk-fuelled fusion , as baritone sax man Ray Murray spits out a message calling for accountability : " We got to police the police . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;In the accompanying video , the band members are clad in prison orange and packed into a paddy wagon as images of police in riot gear and Black Lives Matter protesters scroll by . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " There 's a lot going on in the world , but also stuff happening here in the neighbourhood . It 's a turbulent time , " said Chr ? tien during an interview in the same coffee shop where the confrontation with Abdi started . " Every song is a theory about why the world is in chaos right now . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28592;TOOLONG ... title= " Pierre Chretien , bandleader of Ottawa 's Souljazz Orchestra , October 25 , 2019. " alt= " Pierre Chretien , bandleader of Ottawa 's Souljazz Orchestra , October 25 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;28628;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1493802 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Police The Police was the first song that Chr ? tien , who 's 41 , penned for the new album , a track that launched him and his bandmates off on another round of politically charged songwriting . Also included are the Trump-inspired disco burn of House of Cards , the anti-nuclear War Games and the biting Slumlord , written to express Chr ? tien 's frustration with Ottawa landlords . He 's been renting in the city for more than 20 years , and has experienced a multitude of problems , including rats , bug infestations , and lack of heat . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " We 've had to sleep with mitts and tuques on in the dead of winter because there 's no heat , " says the bandleader , who 's married to Souljazz singer-percussionist Marielle Rivard ; they have a three-year-old son . " We 've had all kinds of stuff , even with the baby . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Souljazz , of course , is known for their social commentary -- ever since their BBC-championed 2007 album , Freedom No Go Die , the band has been trying to make the world a better place by injecting messages into their heady brew of sax-loaded Latin- and jazz-laced funk . Three of their previous albums were nominated for Juno Awards . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Chr ? tien grew up in Sudbury and came to Ottawa to study music at university . The band began in 2002 , evolving during a Thursday-night stint at Babylon nightclub on Bank Street in the early 2000s . The core lineup consists of Chr ? tien on organ , piano and vintage keyboards , with saxmen Zakari Frantz , Steve Patterson , Ray Murray and percussionists Philippe Lafreni ? re and Marielle Rivard . Formerly an instrumental act , the members now share vocal duties . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;When asked what keeps them together , Chr ? tien says it boils down to the fact that everyone gets to be creative . " We 're always changing , trying new things , keeping it fresh , " he says . " It 's not the Pierre project , it 's a collective . I think everyone feels involved and motivated . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;On the new record , another influence that 's bubbling to the surface is Afro-punk , evident in the pointed lyrics and raw , vibrant energy of the music . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " A lot of artists are blending classic punk sounds and attitude with more urban and Afro styles , " Chr ? tien says . " I find it really interesting . It sounds like two things that should n't go together -- funky dance music and punk -- but it kinda works in a weird way . That was the musical inspiration for the album . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The band kicked off the album-release tour last month with a terrific show in front of an enthusiastic crowd of young and older fans at their old stomping ground at Babylon . They 're in the area for two nights this weekend at Wakefield 's Black Sheep Inn , then back on the road for dates in Quebec and Ontario . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;In between the two local venues was a journey to Europe , where the band has a label , gets radio play and regularly sells out concerts . It was a jam-packed adventure that included almost 20 shows from Athens to Dublin in less than a month . During our interview , Chr ? tien was still visibly jetlagged as he talked about the pace of the tour . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " Every night you play until two in the morning , then you 're up at seven to run to the airport to go to the next country , do the soundcheck , play the show , go to bed and get maybe two or three hours of sleep , " he said . " It 's hard to think . It 's like you 're drunk on no sleep . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;One highlight was Paris , where they squeezed two concerts into one evening . The first one was at 9 p.m. in the northern suburbs of Paris , followed by a second one east of the city . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " We packed up as quick as we could and they drove us out to the venue . We just jumped on stage , no sound check or anything , and started performing , " Chr ? tien says . " Sonically I do n't know if it was perfect but by that time we had been on tour for a month so everything was second nature .
