Lynn SaxbergWed , 14 Aug 2019 12:01:03 **45;32143;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusic'I 'm the last one ' : VJ 's piano bar plays its final notehttp : **36;32190;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32228;TOOLONG ... title= " For nearly a quarter century , Viger Gendron owned and operated VJ 's piano bar on Laurier Avenue W. The bar closed last month . " alt= " For nearly a quarter century , Viger Gendron owned and operated VJ 's piano bar on Laurier Avenue W. The bar closed last month . " data-portal-copyright= " Bruce Deachman " **28;32264;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1444585 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Gatineau Club in Aylmer was among the hottest nightclubs in the Ottawa area in 1955 . Duke Ellington performed there that year , as did Josephine Baker , Tony Bennett , Billy Eckstine and Louis Armstrong . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Into this swirling , smoky world of jazz orchestras and pop vocalists , 18-year-old Viger Gendron came of age , waiting on tables and serving drinks while soaking up such hits as Bennett&amp;rsquo ; s Rags to Riches , Ellington&amp;rsquo ; s Satin Doll , and Satchmo&amp;rsquo ; s rendition of I Get Ideas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Because of you , &amp;rdquo ; Gendron sings , imitating Bennett , &amp;ldquo ; there&amp;rsquo ; s a song in my heart . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gendron never abandoned the Great American Songbook , but the public&amp;rsquo ; s appetite for it ebbed . As a result , in late July this year , months shy of its 25th anniversary , Gendron closed his Laurier Avenue piano bar , VJ&amp;rsquo ; s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s the end of an era , &amp;rdquo ; he says , citing other Vegas-style piano bars in the area whose closures preceded his : Friday&amp;rsquo ; s Roast Beef House on Elgin Street ; the Beacon Arms Hotel&amp;rsquo ; s La Bibliotheque on Albert Street . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m the last one . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Surely there are others , but it&amp;rsquo ; s true that the sort of music lounge that Billy Joel sang about in his autobiographical hit song Piano Man has increasingly become an artifact . Today , you can peer through VJ&amp;rsquo ; s windows and see hints of the unpretentious , anachronistic bar that was . A small disco ball hangs from the ceiling , while eight empty chairs remain clustered around a grand piano , as if hoping for customers to call out another tune or put a last bill in the large glass resting atop its quiet lid . The piano&amp;rsquo ; s keys are absent , removed to make room for the electronic keyboard that could be slotted in their place , rendering the grand little more than a large mood-setting drink-holder. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Left to the imagination are the scores of photos of actors and musicians &amp;mdash ; Marilyn Monroe , Clint Eastwood , Clark Gable , Louis Armstrong , even an autographed Sinatra &amp;mdash ; that once covered the forest-green walls . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the floor near the piano , meanwhile , lies a discarded sheet of paper with the lyrics to George and Ira Gershwin&amp;rsquo ; s 1920s standard &amp;lsquo ; S Wonderful printed on it : &amp;ldquo ; You make my life so glamorous . You can&amp;rsquo ; t blame me for feeling amorous ! Wonderful , marvelous , that you should care for me ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over the years , countless people in the mood for a melody came through the doors of VJ&amp;rsquo ; s , where Gendron , quick with a joke or to light up your smoke , held court . Just like at the Gatineau Club so many decades before , he tended bar and served tables . He&amp;rsquo ; d sing , too &amp;mdash ; Engelbert Humperdinck&amp;rsquo ; s After the Lovin&amp;rsquo ; , for example &amp;mdash ; while couples of a certain age danced wherever they could find room . Eventually , however , fewer and fewer people showed up . The Gershwin glamour wore off . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The younger crowd don&amp;rsquo ; t come to piano bars , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s the older , middle-age people , from 50 to 70. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He adds that shorter lunchtimes for nearby public servants , coupled with stricter laws for impaired driving enacted last December , have further quieted business . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s not like it was years ago , when we&amp;rsquo ; d pack them in on Friday nights . Now people have big mortgages and don&amp;rsquo ; t go out so much , and they just talk with each other on their computers and cellphones . And now if you have even one beer and drive , you might be charged . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32294;TOOLONG ... title= " For nearly a quarter century , Viger Gendron owned and operated VJ 's piano bar on Laurier Avenue W. The bar closed last month . " alt= " For nearly a quarter century , Viger Gendron owned and operated VJ 's piano bar on Laurier Avenue W. The bar closed last month . " data-portal-copyright= " Bruce Deachman " **28;32330;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1444587 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; There were other opportunities over the years . Named Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s high school athlete of the year in 1953 , Gendron pursued careers in football and hockey . In 1956 , he played a couple of pre-season games with the Montreal Alouettes before signing with the Sarnia Golden Bears . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the winter he played hockey , initially with Toronto St. Michael&amp;rsquo ; s of the OHA , before moving on to the Hamilton Tiger Cubs , Chatham Maroons and St. Catharines Teepees , at the latter alongside Bobby Hull and Stan Mikita . Hockey eventually took him to the U.S. , where he played for teams in the International , Eastern and Western hockey leagues : The Louisville Rebels , Troy Bruins , Muskegon Zephyrs , Toledo Blades , New Haven Blades , Charlotte Clippers and Los Angeles Blades . A tough blueliner nicknamed &amp;ldquo ; The Tiger&amp;rdquo ; and &amp;ldquo ; Fritz&amp;rdquo ; Gendron spent his share of time in penalty boxes from coast to coast , while a stickfight that cost him a month-long suspension saw him , in 1959 , try his luck with the Wembley Lions of the British National League . The league folded in 1960. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alas , an NHL tryout eluded him . &amp;ldquo ; There were only six teams , &amp;rdquo ; he explains . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the music was never far away . Although not well paid , his hockey career allowed him to spend a summer month one year in Las Vegas , where he attended shows by some of his favourite performers , including Frank Sinatra. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After his hockey career ended , he worked for a while for the Teamsters as a dock supervisor in San Pedro , California . But his wife , Shirley , who died almost four years ago , convinced him to move back to Ottawa , where her family was . And so Gendron , a lifelong non-drinker , returned to work in bars before opening his own . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What now , my love , &amp;rdquo ; he sings , the opening line to Sinatra&amp;rsquo ; s 1966 cover version of the song of the same name . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What now for the nearly 83-year-old Gendron ? He stays in shape and says he could work as a consultant at a gym close to his Centretown apartment . And there&amp;rsquo ; s a nearby bar where he&amp;rsquo ; s been offered night shifts , though he adds he&amp;rsquo ; s had enough of the 12- and 14-hour days that he regularly put in at VJ&amp;rsquo ; s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And while his music is from the past , he says he doesn&amp;rsquo ; t live there , preferring to look forward . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s how you stay young . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And so , to his former customers who may be wondering where he&amp;rsquo ; s gone , he simply offers this : &amp;ldquo ; I might see you again , somewhere . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **30;32360;TOOLONG " **37;32392;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;32431;TOOLONG ... candidates to interview for Ottawa police chief&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;32466;TOOLONG ... double-dealing , death : An excerpt from the mystery novel Spiked&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;32501;TOOLONG ... bar Hooley 's closes , owner blames Elgin Street construction&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Bruce DeachmanMon , 12 Aug 2019 10:49:58 **45;32536;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicChamberfest preview : Pianist Hinrich Alpers is ready to run the Beethoven marathonhttp : **32;32583;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32617;TOOLONG ... title= " Pianist Hinrich Alpers plays Beethoven sonatas July 30 and Aug. 1 in Carleton Dominion-Chalmers Centre . " alt= " Pianist Hinrich Alpers plays Beethoven sonatas July 30 and Aug. 1 in Carleton Dominion-Chalmers Centre . " data-portal-copyright= " Hannes Caspar " **28;32653;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1434976 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You may find it hard to believe , but as a teenager I really didn&amp;rsquo ; t enjoy playing Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s piano music all that much , &amp;rdquo ; says German pianist Hinrich Alpers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fortunately Alpers has seen the light . After all , the Berlin-based 38-year-old will play two &amp;ldquo ; marathon&amp;rdquo ; concerts of Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s piano sonatas at this year&amp;rsquo ; s Chamberfest , performing 15 of the master&amp;rsquo ; s piano sonatas over two afternoons next week . Alpers , an audience favourite over the years at the festival is to return next year and the year after to complete his overview of the Beethoven sonatas. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below , Alpers offers his take on Beethoven , Ottawa audiences and what he&amp;rsquo ; s been cooking lately . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Q : What are your earliest memories of Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s music ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : &amp;ldquo ; My&amp;rdquo ; beloved Beethoven came as a music cassette for Christmas when I had just started to play the piano , and it told the story of Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s life and his music . As it happens , it may have influenced me rather subconsciously , as I am now myself giving concerts and talks about Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s life and works all the time ! I particularly remember the &amp;ldquo ; Thunderstorm&amp;rdquo ; movement of the Sixth Symphony . It amazed me so much that the Sixth is now my favourite symphony . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Q : What place do Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s sonatas have your repertoire ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : The sonatas are , of course , a cornerstone in piano literature , and as such I love them all dearly ! The whole cycle of Beethoven sonatas holds a special place in my musical universe because it enables us&amp;nbsp ; &amp;mdash ; the audience and myself &amp;mdash ; to go on a time journey through Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s life together . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From the very beginning of his career all the way into his 50s ( the so-called &amp;ldquo ; late&amp;rdquo ; Beethoven appreciated all over the world ) , he composed piano sonatas , and I like to follow him on that particular path through his life again and again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This year in Ottawa , we&amp;rsquo ; ll make the first half of that journey together , with the rest to follow next year and the year after that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Q : Do you have any concerns about the &amp;ldquo ; marathon&amp;rdquo ; nature of the concerts ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I&amp;rsquo ; m not concerned at all because with the two intermissions each and my introductions in between I don&amp;rsquo ; t see them really as &amp;ldquo ; marathon&amp;rdquo ; concerts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But you are mentioning a very interesting point , as I have heard actual marathon Beethoven sonatas performances &amp;mdash ; all sonatas within a day . While being rather a sportive achievement , I doubt that those have succeeded quite as much artistically , which is why I am giving some room to breathe in between . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Q : You have been a regular visitor to perform in Ottawa for more than a decade . What do you think of the audiences here ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : What a lovely chance for me to return the many compliments that I&amp;rsquo ; ve received during the past 11 years ! Well , audiences in Ottawa are just terrific , as they are not only friendly and welcoming but also so knowledgeable and patient . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; During my very first year in Ottawa , I had an encounter that sums up the entire spirit of the Ottawa Chamberfest and its audience to me . It was my first concert , and I went for my dress rehearsal at St. John the Evangelist , in pouring rain , and saw a long line of people in front of the door , all in raincoats and with umbrellas . At first I believed I had arrived at the wrong place . But then I was told they&amp;rsquo ; d all been waiting for an hour already in order to get the best seats to hear my performance . Isn&amp;rsquo ; t that lovely ? Ottawa Chamberfest and I were love at first sight ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Q : Your website notes that you listen to everything from Renaissance choral music to 21st century jazz . What appeals to you about these kinds of music that you don&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily play ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Every bit of music that I hear might eventually reflect in an interpretation of something else . I have always loved to listen to jazz , and for my own pleasure I also play quite a bit of it . There&amp;rsquo ; s an element of fun and implicitness in it which I believe I&amp;rsquo ; d like to allow to rub off on my actual &amp;ldquo ; classic&amp;rdquo ; pianism which I perform in public . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Naturally , while I studied in New York , I found myself in a rather perfect environment &amp;mdash ; I went to hear many of my jazz musician friends&amp;rsquo ; performances , and they&amp;rsquo ; re also the artists that I still follow and listen to . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Q : Your website also says that you enjoy culinary pursuits . What have you been up to in the kitchen ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I have started to dig into some Vietnamese cooking ! Having been there a few times , there are some flavours that I felt I needed more regularly , and over the internet , many of the recipes for my favourite dishes are easy to find . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Essential Beethoven : Hinrich Alpers &lt;br&gt; When : Tuesday , July 30 , at 1 p.m. and Thursday , Aug. 1 , at 1 p.m. &lt;br&gt; Where : Carleton Dominion-Chalmers Centre &lt;br&gt; Tickets and info : &lt;a href= " http : //chamberfest.com " &gt;chamberfest. ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;br&gt; twitter.com/jazzblog &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;32683;TOOLONG ... preview : St. Lawrence String Quartet promises ' stupendous tour de force of pure fun ' &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;32718;TOOLONG ... preview : Eclectic pianist Stephen Prutsman stages concerts for children on the autism spectrum&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;32753;TOOLONG ... story-teller ' picked to create public art at new central library&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 26 Jul 2019 19:22:54 **45;32788;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicChamberfest preview : St. Lawrence String Quartet promises ' stupendous tour de force of pure fun'http : **36;32835;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32873;TOOLONG ... title= " The St. Lawrence String Quartet will perform at 2019 Chamberfest. " alt= " The St. Lawrence String Quartet will perform at 2019 Chamberfest. " data-portal-copyright= " Marco Borggreve " **28;32909;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1431088 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Geoff Nuttall , first violinist and co-founder of the St. Lawrence String Quartet , recalls one of the nicest things said to him during his ensemble&amp;rsquo ; s storied 30-year history . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; One of the best compliments I ever had was from a quasi-homeless dude in Berkeley , &amp;rdquo ; Nuttall remembers . &amp;ldquo ; We played a show and he was like , &amp;lsquo ; You remind me of a kid . &amp;rsquo ; I think that&amp;rsquo ; s a good thing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His elaboration takes some time to unfurl , but it&amp;rsquo ; s easy to bear with Nuttall because in an interview , he sounds almost as passionate about music as he does when he&amp;rsquo ; s bowing his instrument on stage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Robert Mann of the Juilliard String Quartet , one of my heroes , would always say you&amp;rsquo ; ve got to play like you&amp;rsquo ; re seeing a score for the first time , &amp;rdquo ; Nuttall continues . He then points out the inherent contradiction in achieving this feat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But of course , you&amp;rsquo ; ve already rehearsed that section 500 times to get it to a point where it feels comfortable about what you&amp;rsquo ; re doing to it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For all quartets , there&amp;rsquo ; s no escaping the thousands of hours of work it takes to get to a baseline level . Then you have to forget all that and make it seem like the excitement of discovery for the first time &amp;hellip ; The great performers make that happen . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;32939;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;32975;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Founded in Toronto in 1989 by Nuttall and viola player Lesley Robertson soon after they graduated from the University of Toronto , the St. Lawrence String Quartet has been acclaimed for the intensity and drama of its music-making . The quartet can even bring &amp;ldquo ; a hint of rock &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; roll energy to Haydn and Beethoven , &amp;rdquo ; a Los Angeles Times reviewer wrote several years ago . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; An ensemble in residence at Stanford University in California since 1998 , the quartet during its three decades has frequently made the trip to Ottawa to perform . It returns this week to star in Chamberfest&amp;rsquo ; s first big concert at Carleton Dominion-Chalmers Centre on Thursday , when works by 18th-century Austrian composer Joseph Haydn , the father of string quartet music , 19th-century composer C&amp;eacute ; sar Franck and contemporary composer Bryce Dessner will be on the program . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Of course , we have to play Haydn because he&amp;rsquo ; s the best , &amp;rdquo ; Nuttall says . &amp;ldquo ; If you&amp;rsquo ; re playing string quartets , Haydn is the man . Opus 75 No. 5 is from the height of his powers . It&amp;rsquo ; s just effortless and inspiring and imaginative and moving and fun . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Saturday afternoon , the quartet will play another Haydn string quartet at the National Gallery of Canada , but only after discussing the work and giving listeners a peek under the hood regarding what the piece involves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nuttall says the quartet began combining education with performance to help listeners better connect with the greatness of Haydn . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We play Haydn and we think it&amp;rsquo ; s the greatest thing ever &amp;mdash ; and students would not share our enthusiasm , &amp;rdquo ; Nuttall says . &amp;ldquo ; We figured we were doing something wrong , because it&amp;rsquo ; s not Haydn&amp;rsquo ; s fault . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If people aren&amp;rsquo ; t really excited , the hair&amp;rsquo ; s not standing up on the backs of their necks , they&amp;rsquo ; re not amused and shocked , and it&amp;rsquo ; s just such incredible music &amp;hellip ; we were like , &amp;lsquo ; What&amp;rsquo ; s going on ? &amp;rsquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Haydn suffers more than any composer from lazy listening &amp;mdash ; the **35;33011;TOOLONG syndrome , where you put it on at cocktail parties and everyone feels smarter and calmer , &amp;rdquo ; Nuttall continues . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s totally not the point . It actually works , but if you don&amp;rsquo ; t actually listen , you miss a lot of the jokes , you miss a lot of the musical connections . You miss a lot of the imaginative twists and turns of motivic development . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Hopefully people ( after the talk ) will listen in a more engaged and active way . Hopefully it opens doors &amp;mdash ; &amp;lsquo ; Hey , I want to hear more Haydn , I want to play more Haydn . ' &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;33048;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;33084;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; After its Thursday night concert , the quartet will play a late-night show that&amp;rsquo ; s typical of Chamberfest&amp;rsquo ; s more eclectic 10 p.m. programming . Then , the group will be part of pianist Steven Prutsman&amp;rsquo ; s live accompaniment for the Buster Keaton silent film College . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a stupendous tour de force of pure fun , and the movie&amp;rsquo ; s hilarious too , &amp;rdquo ; says Nuttall . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nuttall says he approaches all of the quartet&amp;rsquo ; s concerts with the same commitment , regardless of material and venue . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We just did a show outside for a bunch of families and a lot of young kids in Montana . To me , it&amp;rsquo ; s the same same sort of focus and preparation that goes into a Wigmore Hall show , &amp;rdquo ; says Nuttall , referring to the famed Victorian concert hall in London , England . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; " A concert is a concert basically , whether it 's a talk-and-play for a bunch of kindergarteners or a three-hour marathon of all six Opus 20 quartets for a bunch of hardcore string quartet fans in London . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For that matter , rehearsing is a pleasure and not a chore , he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s like , wow , we get to spend the time to break this apart and discover new things about it , &amp;rdquo ; Nuttall says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a joy . If you don&amp;rsquo ; t have joy in the act of rehearsing , and the act of connecting to the audiences , there&amp;rsquo ; s no way you&amp;rsquo ; re going to give the music its just due . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nuttall acknowledges that rehearsing for a string quartet has a special intensity that can take a toll on relations among the musicians . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You hear all sorts of quartet stories about traveling separately and not speaking to each other , &amp;rdquo ; says Nuttall . &amp;ldquo ; I think we&amp;rsquo ; re pretty normal . We spent a lot of time together in the last three weeks , riding around in minivans . We&amp;rsquo ; re getting along pretty well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The goal is to get better &amp;mdash ; which means there&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of criticism involved , being self-critical , or being critical to your colleagues , saying , &amp;lsquo ; Hey , we&amp;rsquo ; re not in tune here , let&amp;rsquo ; s figure it out . &amp;rsquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s going to result in people&amp;rsquo ; s egos being bruised . Otherwise you just won&amp;rsquo ; t get better . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; All the great musicians I&amp;rsquo ; ve ever hung out with &amp;hellip ; they had that combination , &amp;rdquo ; Nuttall says . &amp;ldquo ; You&amp;rsquo ; ve got to be mercilessly self-critical and you have to have the enthusiasm of a kid . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;St . Lawrence String Quartet and Friends&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When and Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Thursday , June 25 , 7 p.m. , Carleton Dominion-Chalmers Centre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Live Track : Buster Keaton 's College&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When and Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Thursday , June 25 , 10 p.m. , Carleton Dominion-Chalmers Centre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Haydn Discovery&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When and Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Saturday , June 27 , 1 p.m. , National Gallery of Canada &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Info and tickets&lt;/strong&gt; : &lt;a href= " http : //chamberfest.com " &gt;chamberfest. ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;33120;TOOLONG " **32;33147;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;33181;TOOLONG ... preview : St. Lawrence String Quartet promises ' stupendous tour de force of pure fun ' &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;33216;TOOLONG ... future of health care has arrived -- but is the Ontario government paying attention ? &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;33251;TOOLONG ... story-teller ' picked to create public art at new central library&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 25 Jul 2019 15:11:41 **45;33286;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicPoor ticket sales forced cancellation of Nordstock , organizer sayshttp : **36;33333;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;33371;TOOLONG ... title= " microphones on stage " alt= " microphones on stage " data-portal-copyright= " Ssorca " **28;33407;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1434791 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Nordstock festival , intended as a celebration of the 50th anniversary of the famed Woodstock festival , has been cancelled , with executive director Timothy Laflamme admitting he was in over his head . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 33-year-old first-time festival organizer said there wasn&amp;rsquo ; t enough money from ticket sales to cover the costs associated with mounting a festival . Nordstock was to be held Aug. 9-11 at Lansdowne Park , featuring a roster of cover bands paying tribute to the artists who played the original Woodstock . Tickets went on sale about eight months ago . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Unfortunately , the ticket sales weren&amp;rsquo ; t there to help balance out all the deposits that were due to the city , and the sound and lighting and stage ( companies ) , &amp;rdquo ; Laflamme said in an interview . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s the sad reality of it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He said it became evident Tuesday that the costs would be impossible to cover . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When they come calling for hundreds of thousands of dollars , I don&amp;rsquo ; t have that kind of money and sales were not satisfactory . It became too large of a hill to climb . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Announced last fall as Woodstock North , the organizing team initially included Laflamme , Fenton Brothers&amp;rsquo ; guitarist John Fenton , who was booking the bands , and his friend , Larry Johnson , a retired businessman . With a decade or so as a festival volunteer , Laflamme was the only one with hands-on experience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the event was soon clouded by bad luck , starting with a cease-and-desist letter from the original Woodstock stakeholders on the use of the Woodstock name . In January , the Ottawa event was renamed Nordstock to avoid legal action . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then , by spring , Fenton was diagnosed with stage 4 liver cancer and stepped away from the planning , followed by Johnson . Laflamme was determined to continue . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We all had a big dream , &amp;rdquo ; Laflamme said , &amp;ldquo ; but some of us decided to leave because they couldn&amp;rsquo ; t put the energy in it anymore . There&amp;rsquo ; s no hard feelings , but because we had taken people&amp;rsquo ; s money and made commitments , I wanted to persevere . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Laflamme said he put more than $15,000 of his own money into the operation in the last five weeks to cover marketing and staffing costs . &amp;ldquo ; I took the reins and pushed as hard as I could in every facet , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ticket refunds will be available at point of purchase . A market area had also been planned , with vendors paying a $200 registration fee to participate . Laflamme hopes that money can be refunded , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the end , Laflamme called it a huge learning experience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We had a great vision for what it could be , and we just didn&amp;rsquo ; t have the resources to execute that vision , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; To be honest , I&amp;rsquo ; m extremely disappointed in myself for not being able to complete the project and I feel bad for everybody who made plans to come . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;33437;TOOLONG ... wish I could n't remember ' : Driver recounts fatal collision with 13-year-old cyclist&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;33472;TOOLONG ... story-teller ' picked to create public art at new central library&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;33507;TOOLONG ... residents demand sidewalk be reopened at Magee House&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;33542;TOOLONG " **36;33573;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Lynn SaxbergThu , 25 Jul 2019 00:35:41 **45;33611;TOOLONG ... to grow a music festival : Kemptville fest thrives on boomershttp : **33;33658;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;33693;TOOLONG ... title= " Kim Mitchell . " alt= " Kim Mitchell . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;33729;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1429317 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Between shrinking budgets and rising costs , these are challenging times for Ontario music festivals . It 's difficult to start an event , and even more difficult to keep it going year after year . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The Kemptville Live Music Festival is one of the rare success stories among young festivals in the province . The fifth annual edition of the event takes place July 18-21 , and it 's shaping up to be the biggest yet . Tickets are going fast , with attendance expected to hit the 25,000 mark over the weekend . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Certainly the main attraction is the lineup , which in past years has brought major names such as Blue Rodeo , Tom Cochrane , Gordon Lightfoot and the Beach Boys to Kemptville , a small town less than an hour 's drive south of Ottawa . Programming the lineup is the work of artistic director &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Bob Beshara , who founded the festival in 2015. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;He 's done a terrific job this year , too , with a classic rock-heavy assemblage of dad faves anchored by George Thorogood , Steve Earle and the Dukes , April Wine and Kim Mitchell . It 's a make-up date for Mitchell , who was originally scheduled to perform at the festival last year , but had to cancel for medical reasons . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Of course , bands of a certain era appeal to fans of a certain age , and catering to them is a key component of this festival 's popularity . According to executive director Karen Bedard , most people who buy tickets to Kemptville Live are in the 45-65 plus age bracket , with the over-65 demographic growing the fastest . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " It changes a lot of factors , " Bedard said in an interview . " Accessibility is one of the things we 've identified as a priority . There are a lot more accessibility needs in that demographic . We know our people wo n't be standing . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The concert site on the grounds of Kemptville College is designed for accessibility , allowing more room per person than the one-metre square usually allocated for crowd-planning purposes . Boomer-aged festivalgoers need about three times that much space to accommodate lawnchairs , umbrellas and picnic baskets , and possibly also strollers , wheelchairs or other medical devices . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Without public transit in Kemptville , organizers offer plenty of free parking in the surrounding area and provide shuttles to the site . Drop-off zones and complimentary golf-cart shuttles are available , too . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;New this year is a second , on-site bar so patrons do n't have to stand in line as long to buy a beer . There 's also a smoking area where cannabis and vaping will be allowed . The campgrounds have been expanded , too . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Kemptville Live has been lucky with Celebrate Ontario grants , this year receiving about $80,000 from the province , down from last year 's $100,000 . The money is intended to encourage tourism initiatives . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Bedard says the festival draws more people from out of town every year . While most come from other Ontario communities , a growing number come from Canada 's Maritime provinces , the eastern United States and beyond , including a handful from the United Kingdom . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Still , making ends meet is a challenge for the non-profit organization . Inclement weather could sink the budget , and it 's a stretch to ask small , local businesses for more money every year . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " Funding is always a risk , " Bedard said . " We recognize that , and it was part of our first five-year plan . We do have some great local sponsors , but we anticipated that funding would go down over time so we have to make sure we rely on ticket sales . I&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;t would only take one bad weekend to lose a lot of money . We reinvest any earnings we have right back into the organization and sock it away for next year . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; lsaxberg@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; **34;33759;TOOLONG Live Music **34;33795;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Where and when : &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;July 18-21 , Kemptville College grounds , 830 Prescott St. , Kemptville&lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Tickets : $60 each day or $165 for a weekend pass , available at **31;33831;TOOLONG . ArtsMusicBluesfest rolls with the punches as it closes out its 25th anniversary editionhttp : **33;34055;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;34090;TOOLONG ... title= " The crowd at Bluesfest on Sunday night . Executive director Mark Monahan said estimates were that overall attendance for 2019 would match last year 's figures . " alt= " The crowd at Bluesfest on Sunday night . Executive director Mark Monahan said estimates were that overall attendance for 2019 would match last year 's figures . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;34126;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1428863 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bluesfest rolled with the punches thrown its way for its 25th anniversary edition , with attendance figures matching last year&amp;rsquo ; s , according to executive director Mark Monahan , despite a couple nights of wicked weather and a &amp;ldquo ; bad string of luck&amp;rdquo ; that led to some last-minute artist cancellations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I find the anniversaries are tricky to figure out how to celebrate . But we take it as a marker to look back and celebrate what we&amp;rsquo ; ve accomplished , to thank the people who have contributed to the festival over the years and to reminisce a little bit , &amp;rdquo ; Monahan said on Bluesfest&amp;rsquo ; s closing day , with organizers expecting its biggest crowd of the festival for the Sunday night extravaganza with Backstreet Boys . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There were the usual thematic links Monahan and his programming team try to match up &amp;mdash ; with one night featuring indie rock , another night country , hip-hop , blues , punk and hardcore&amp;nbsp ; &amp;mdash ; to reach as many fans of all musical stripes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our programming team are really trying to be more proactive on developing those themes and trying to figure out how things will work . On Tuesday , we had Buddy Guy ( on the Videotron Stage ) , the Trews on the Bluesville Stage and we had KYGO on the City Stage , and , really , that&amp;rsquo ; s a perfect example of where we had all three stages full &amp;mdash ; with the appropriate amount of people &amp;mdash ; and all three shows were great . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t always work that way . There&amp;rsquo ; s always a few pieces we don&amp;rsquo ; t get quite right , and some others we hit a home run . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34156;TOOLONG ... title= " Bluesfest is delayed once again on Day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. " alt= " Bluesfest is delayed once again on Day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;34192;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1427432 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bluesfest was thrown a few curveballs that sent the organizing team scrambling into action , whether related to foul weather or other assorted circumstances . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Colter Wall cancelled his appearance in the opening week , Jesse Reyes ( injury ) and Elle King ( personal reasons ) were both late cancellations on Wednesday , and on Pauly D cancelled Thursday due to weather-related issues as a storm wiped out that evening&amp;rsquo ; s opening acts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; First Aid Kit was one of the top-billed acts when the lineup was announced in January , but the Swedish sister act cancelled the leg of their tour that would have brought them to Canada&amp;rsquo ; s capital . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We seem to have had a bad string of luck this year , &amp;rdquo ; Monahan said . &amp;ldquo ; But , on the other side of it , we always try to add bands as well &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; alt-J wasn&amp;rsquo ; t on the original lineup , we had Warren G. with Snoop Dogg , so there&amp;rsquo ; s all sorts of things we add , but , unfortunately , there are also things that don&amp;rsquo ; t end up happening . And this year it was for different reasons , but reasons beyond our control . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When you book a hundred or so bands , the odds are one or two or a few of them are going to be unable to make it , &amp;rdquo ; Monahan said . &amp;ldquo ; If we can replace these bands , we try to , but it&amp;rsquo ; s not a great situation . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34222;TOOLONG ... title= " Bands were called off all the stages due to severe weather in the area during Bluesfest , Saturday , July 13 , 2019. " alt= " Bands were called off all the stages due to severe weather in the area during Bluesfest , Saturday , July 13 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;34258;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1428525 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The management team also had plenty of practice at dealing with sudden changes in the weather , and that went into effect again Saturday , when another thunderstorm threatened to cut short performances by Wu-Tang Clan and Snoop Dogg. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both shows went on , but started late , and the music finished well past the 11 p.m. cutoff after organizers gave city officials a heads-up , then approached each performer about a revised schedule . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s never perfect , and , every time we have ( weather issues ) like this , we learn from it , and we just have to manage on the fly and do our best , &amp;rdquo ; Monahan said . &amp;ldquo ; First and foremost is to err on the side of caution , and , once you&amp;rsquo ; re clear , do what you can to get everyone on . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Monahan listed several personal highlights , including one of the festival&amp;rsquo ; s few forays into world music with The Turbans , who whipped an enthusiastic crowd into a frenzy on the opening weekend . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34288;TOOLONG ... title= " The Turbans performing at RBC Ottawa Bluesfest on July 6 , 2019 . PHOTO : Greg Kolz " alt= " The Turbans performing at RBC Ottawa Bluesfest on July 6 , 2019 . PHOTO : Greg Kolz " data-portal-copyright= " Greg Kolz " **28;34324;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1428879 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They were a real pleasant surprise , &amp;rdquo ; Monahan said . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve tried to do more world music with , honestly , not a lot of luck . But , seeing that , I thought we should be trying to do more , and there was an amazing audience for that show , who really appreciated it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Monahan listed artists like Jason Isbell as ones he was finally able to cross off his &amp;ldquo ; bucket list , &amp;rdquo ; but pointed to upstart Ottawa acts like BlakDenim and Sly High as some of his most pleasant surprises . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s some great music going on locally , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Our folks like ( festival programmers ) Kelly Symes and Emma Francis are really in tune with the local scene and they really try to maximize the exposure for these bands , to put them on the right stage in the right spot to blend in with the theme for the night . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now that Bluesfest has reached its finale for this festival season , the team gets a short break before CityFolk rolls in to Lansdowne Park on Sept. 12 to 15 , featuring Robert Plant , Leon Bridges and Our Lady Peace . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While both festivals received their share of provincial funding this year , even after the 2018 change in government , other festivals weren&amp;rsquo ; t so fortunate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a really difficult situation this year , obviously , for a lot of festivals that got cut back or didn&amp;rsquo ; t receive funding , and it&amp;rsquo ; s really unfortunate , &amp;rdquo ; Monahan said . &amp;ldquo ; We were lucky to receive our funding and I hope that the province can figure out a way to make it up to the festivals that were cut back &amp;hellip ; fairly quickly , or at the last minute . That&amp;rsquo ; s not a great situation , but we are appreciative of what we get , and we&amp;rsquo ; ll continue to work hard to hopefully continue that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;34354;TOOLONG " **35;34384;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Twitter : &lt;a href= " https : //twitter.com/helmera " &gt;@helmera&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;34421;TOOLONG ... airlifted with life-threatening injuries after crash near Cardinal&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;34457;TOOLONG ... search underway for missing teens in Algonquin Park&lt;/a&gt; &lt;strong&gt; &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;34492;TOOLONG ... injuries sustained in Saturday night shooting near Hunt Club Road&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Aedan HelmerMon , 15 Jul 2019 12:40:49 **45;34527;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicBluesfest Review : Snoop Dogg , Wu-Tang Clan weather the stormshttp : **34;34574;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;34610;TOOLONG ... title= " Snoop Dogg played on the City Stage at Bluesfest , Saturday , July 13 , 2019. " alt= " Snoop Dogg played on the City Stage at Bluesfest , Saturday , July 13 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;34646;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1428576 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;34676;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Snoop Dogg / Wu-Tang Clan &lt;br&gt; RBC Ottawa Bluesfest &lt;br&gt; Saturday &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was two of hip-hop&amp;rsquo ; s heaviest hitters from coast-to-coast squaring off on Bluesfest&amp;rsquo ; s main stage Saturday with Staten Island&amp;rsquo ; s legendary Wu-Tang Clan up against Long Beach , California&amp;rsquo ; s top dog , Snoop Dogg. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A wicked thunderstorm blew in halfway through a rousing set by Canadian hip-hop icon K&amp;rsquo ; Naan , washing out the rest of the evening programming and forcing festival organizers to scramble the schedule to ensure both Wu-Tang and Snoop got their share of the spotlight . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was just after 9 p.m. &amp;mdash ; about an hour past Wu-Tang&amp;rsquo ; s scheduled start &amp;mdash ; when the show resumed as each of the Clan&amp;rsquo ; s multifaceted MCs took the stage in order . Their leader and visionary RZA called on the crowd repeatedly to show them the &amp;ldquo ; energy&amp;rdquo ; they needed to propel them onstage with all the fanfare you&amp;rsquo ; d expect from the fabled Shaolin-inspired crew . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And the Wu-Tang Clan brought the ruckus from the very first bass drop on Bring Da Ruckus as each of Ghostface Killah , Raekwon and Inspectah Deck stormed on stage in time for each respective verse , and each unleashing rhymes with a fury to match the storm that had just blown through . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Celebrating 25 years since their landmark debut Enter the Wu-Tang ( 36 Chambers ) dropped &amp;mdash ; and making them a perfect pairing with Bluesfest , closing out its own quarter-century celebrations this weekend &amp;mdash ; RZA promised they&amp;rsquo ; d get to play all the songs from that monumental record . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; They did that and more , continuing track-by-track with Shame on a N&amp;mdash ; - , with the late Ol&amp;rsquo ; Dirty Bastard&amp;rsquo ; s portrait on the backdrop in tribute , and his son Young Dirty Bastard now handling his ol&amp;rsquo ; man&amp;rsquo ; s parts with same wild-eyed abandon . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; RZA pumped the crowd up trading rhymes with GZA , and had the crowd raising their hands in the Clan&amp;rsquo ; s trademark &amp;ldquo ; W&amp;rdquo ; formation and chanting &amp;ldquo ; Wu-Tang Forever&amp;rdquo ; across a LeBreton Flats park that quickly filled to brimming once the weather lifted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As if there weren&amp;rsquo ; t enough voices blasting from the silhouetted stage , they invited Cappadonna , &amp;ldquo ; the tenth member of the Wu-Tang&amp;rdquo ; to trade rhymes before veering back on course with Can It Be All So Simple , Da Mystery of Chessboxin , and C.R.E.A.M. , with each corresponding video flashing across the screen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; RZA told his faithful to &amp;ldquo ; never forget this one lesson in hip-hop , &amp;rdquo ; and clearly it was lesson learned as the whole crowd chanted along on Wu-Tang Clan Ain&amp;rsquo ; t Nuthin to F&amp;rsquo ; Wit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That sentiment was self-evident by the time they shared the tale of their beginnings to introduce their ( eventual ) breakout hit Protect Ya Neck . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everything has a beginning , &amp;rdquo ; RZA said , &amp;ldquo ; And for us it was this single that we sold out the back of a truck , that we went from record store to record store to sell . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thankfully that wasn&amp;rsquo ; t the ending as Wu-Tang treated fans to a cover of The Beatles&amp;rsquo ; Come Together , and an unexpectedly faithful take on Nirvana&amp;rsquo ; s Smells Like Teen Spirit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; *** &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No stranger to showing up fashionably late , this time it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t by choice as Snoop Dogg hit the stage just after 10:30 p.m. , and stayed on well past the curfew . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At 15 minutes to midnight , he was still blasting out tunes from House of Pain&amp;rsquo ; s Jump Around to his duet with Wiz Khalifa , Young , Wild and Free , as the refrain that contains the Snoop Dogg credo rang out across the festival grounds to close the night . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So what we get drunk / So what we smoke weed / We&amp;rsquo ; re just having fun / We don&amp;rsquo ; t care who sees . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And just like he did when he rocked Bluesfest&amp;rsquo ; s 2014 edition , Snoop took his fans on a trip through the best of his own catalogue , including his many collaborations , along with a string of handpicked hits from his own favourites . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tha Shiznit , DJ Khaled&amp;rsquo ; s All I Do Is Win , and 50 Cent&amp;rsquo ; s P.I.M.P. opened the show in short order before he brought out his buddy Warren G. to trade verses on This DJ and Do You See . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He tipped his hat to Dr. Dre on The Next Episode and Nuthin&amp;rsquo ; But a G Thang , and always lived up to the lyric he sang on So Fly : &amp;ldquo ; So fly , Super fly . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Any time you want us to come back , &amp;rdquo ; he promised at the end . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bluesfest&amp;rsquo ; s grand finale comes Sunday with Backstreet Boys closing out the 25 **25;34712;TOOLONG anniversary edition . &lt;/p&gt; ReviewsMusicBluesfest Review : Kane Brown , Gone West make the most of a soggy festival nighthttp : **35;34891;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;34928;TOOLONG ... title= " Gone West , composed of ( from left ) Jason Reeves , Nelly Joy , Colbie Caillat and Justin Young , performing on the city stage following a severe thunderstorm that blew through Ottawa on day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. " alt= " Gone West , composed of ( from left ) Jason Reeves , Nelly Joy , Colbie Caillat and Justin Young , performing on the city stage following a severe thunderstorm that blew through Ottawa on day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;34964;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1427531 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;34994;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Kane Brown / Gone West feat . Colbie Caillat&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;RBC Ottawa Bluesfest&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; **37;35030;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bluesfest salvaged a soggy night Thursday as fast-rising crossover country star Kane Brown delivered a stirring show that threatened to be washed out earlier by a torrential evening thunderstorm. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The skies cleared in time , though , and the crowd finally turned up to a sopping wet LeBreton Flats as the 25-year-old Brown came out atop the laser-lit stage , dressed in all-black with a black ball cap sporting his own &amp;ldquo ; KB&amp;rdquo ; brand as the strains of the Lil Nas X hit Old Town Road faded from the pre-show loudspeakers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Backed by a crack six-piece band , featuring the traditional country sounds of acoustic guitar , banjo and fiddle , with a jolt of electricity courtesy of some hard-rocking electric guitar , bass and drums , the band cooked behind Brown&amp;rsquo ; s twangy Georgia baritone voice on the opening hit Baby Come Back to Me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35069;TOOLONG ... title= " Kane Brown . " alt= " Kane Brown . " data-portal-copyright= " HANDOUT " **28;35105;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1425793 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The nice acoustic and banjo riff that kicked off What Ifs was soon swallowed up by the booming rock n&amp;rsquo ; roll rhythm section as Brown gave a shout-out to all the &amp;ldquo ; party people&amp;rdquo ; in the crowd . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It took awhile for the party to really get started , though , after a rainstorm and circling lightning washed out Thursday&amp;rsquo ; s evening opening acts and forced the festival to delay its start by an hour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Several acts were cancelled , including Gavin McLeod , Abby Stewart and local gal Brea Lawrenson&amp;rsquo ; s opening set on the City Stage , while DJ Pauly D cancelled his headlining set on the Videotron Stage due to weather-related travel issues . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The weather actually let us play ! &amp;rdquo ; Brown cheered after showing off a video montage during Weekend with him and his pals hanging out at a lakeside barbecue. &amp;ldquo ; I thought I was gon na have to cancel , and I don&amp;rsquo ; t get to come up to Canada all that often . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He showed just how much he appreciated the love the fans gave him as he thanked &amp;ldquo ; everybody who got me on stage today&amp;rdquo ; on Used to Love You Sober , with a hot fiddle solo that gave way to the sing along chorus , as Brown turned the mic over to the crowd to take over the vocals. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The night&amp;rsquo ; s most tender moment came with Brown sharing the story of his tough upbringing in the small town of Fort Oglethorpe , Georgia , with an abusive stepfather and his experiences with racism &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; The type of stuff that&amp;rsquo ; ll put a hole in your soul , &amp;rdquo ; as he sang on Learning , a song that chronicles his early struggles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t know how it is up here but I just think racism should go to hell , &amp;rdquo ; he said to a loud cheer . &amp;ldquo ; For anyone suffering out there , just know you can make it out . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brown dedicated the ballad Homesick to the troops overseas and showed a montage of U.S. soldiers sending well-wishes back home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But he soon traded the serious tone for more lighthearted party fare on Beautiful Girls , and showed he was equally at ease handling hits of any era on a mash-up of Ben E. King&amp;rsquo ; s classic Stand By Me , meandering unexpectedly into Marshmello&amp;rsquo ; s cheesy pop hit Happier . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Gone West feat . Colbie Caillat&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The evening rainfall didn&amp;rsquo ; t deter the few hundred brave , soggy souls who showed up early for Gone West , as the soothing Nashville foursome made sure to show their appreciation for those who stuck it out through the storm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That storm was wild and crazy and we didn&amp;rsquo ; t know if anyone was gon na show up , &amp;rdquo ; said singer Colbie Caillat as she told the modern day Music City love story of how their band came together . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35135;TOOLONG ... title= " Gone West performing on the city stage following a severe thunderstorm that blew through Ottawa on day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. " alt= " Gone West performing on the city stage following a severe thunderstorm that blew through Ottawa on day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;35171;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1427533 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As the story goes , Caillat met fellow songwriter Jason Reeves 14 years ago in Nashville and began co-writing her 2007 debut Coco , featuring her breakout hit single Bubbly , which she introduced as a tune written by a bright-eyed 20-year-old pondering &amp;ldquo ; the idea of love and what I hoped it would feel like . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those hopes came true when she met Hawaiian songwriter Justin Young , and as Reeves explained in the intro to Realize , he may have had something to do with hooking them up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Caillat and Reeves were co-writing that tune , Reeves &amp;ldquo ; snuck in&amp;rdquo ; a few lyrics about &amp;ldquo ; a friend of mine who had a giant crush on her , and maybe if she listened to the words she&amp;rsquo ; d realize she liked him too . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And I did , &amp;rdquo ; Caillat chimed in with a wink at Young , her musical and romantic muse of the past 10 years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35201;TOOLONG ... title= " Gone West members ( from left ) Nelly Joy and Colbie Caillat performing on the city stage following a severe thunderstorm that blew through Ottawa on day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. " alt= " Gone West members ( from left ) Nelly Joy and Colbie Caillat performing on the city stage following a severe thunderstorm that blew through Ottawa on day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;35237;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1427543 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The same year they met and fell in love , in 2009 , Reeves began co-writing with a Nashville duo called The JaneDear Girls , featuring Nelly Joy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was love at first write &amp;hellip ; for him , &amp;rdquo ; Joy joked , with a playful nudge to Reeves , now her husband of the past seven years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Fleetwood Mac comparisons are obvious &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s fun being two couples in a band , &amp;rdquo ; Caillat said at one point &amp;mdash ; but the similarities were evident on stage even before she explained the romantic intertwinings behind the music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Slow Down , with its cool , breezy chorus , slinky electric guitar hook and gorgeous four-part harmonies wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t have sounded out of place on Rumours . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35267;TOOLONG ... title= " Gone West members ( from left ) Jason Reeves and Nelly Joy performing on the city stage following a severe thunderstorm that blew through Ottawa on day 7 of RBC Bluesfest. ArtsMusicCatch them while you can : Hollerado set to call it quitshttp : **35;35618;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;35655;TOOLONG ... title= " Ottawa 's Hollerado starts a major cross-Canada tour this week . " alt= " Ottawa 's Hollerado starts a major cross-Canada tour this week . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35691;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1003305 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **38;35721;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;5:30 p.m . Saturday , HOPE Volleyball Summerfest , Mooney 's Bay Park&lt;/em&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Also performing : Beaches , 4 p.m. , and Elijah Woods x Jamie Fine , 2:30 p.m. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Tickets : $20 advance , available at hopehelps.ca , or $27 at the gate . Children nine and under are admitted free . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After 12 years as a band , Hollerado has decided to call it quits . Formed in Manotick by &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;singer-songwriter ... Menno Versteeg and his neighbourhood pals Jake and Nixon Boyd , with Nepean 's Dean Baxter on bass&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; , Hollerado is wrapping things up at the end of this year . A final string of tour dates is in the works , likely including Ottawa in the fall , and the fun-loving indie-rockers just released a final album , Retaliation Vacation . In the meantime , they 're playing HOPE Volleyball Summerfest on Saturday . Here 's more on the end of an era from the 39-year-old Versteeg : &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Hollerado is pulling the plug ! What prompted this decision ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Nothing juicy . We&amp;rsquo ; re all still best friends ; it&amp;rsquo ; s not like we hate each other . Exactly the opposite . We&amp;rsquo ; ve had such a great time playing together for 14 years , and we just feel that this project has done what it needs to do . It&amp;rsquo ; s not like the money is good enough to do something you can&amp;rsquo ; t stand behind now , which a lot of bands do . &amp;lsquo ; My kid needs braces so I&amp;rsquo ; m going to keep playing shows . &amp;rsquo ; A lot of these songs we wrote 14 years ago , and we&amp;rsquo ; ve changed a lot in that time . It doesn&amp;rsquo ; t seem right to keep singing them if your heart isn&amp;rsquo ; t entirely behind it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : So your heart is not entirely behind it ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : It&amp;rsquo ; s not that I don&amp;rsquo ; t like the songs . We all like the songs , it&amp;rsquo ; s just ( that ) music is something we&amp;rsquo ; re doing for ourselves as much as the audience . When you&amp;rsquo ; re up there every night singing a song about yourself 15 years ago , it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to move on past that . We want to express the people we are now . We&amp;rsquo ; re all making things or working on music . Everyone is playing music like crazy . It&amp;rsquo ; s not like we&amp;rsquo ; re stopping &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Plus you run a label , Royal Mountain Records , featuring some great young acts like PUP and Dizzy . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Oh yeah , that&amp;rsquo ; s the other thing . A big part of the job taking up a lot of my time , in a good way , is helping other bands get their tour off the ground , which is a really rewarding thing in itself . I enjoy working with developing bands just starting their career , and helping them not make the mistakes I made . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : There 's also a new Hollerado record , and you guys sound invigorated on it . What did you set out to do with this one ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : We **25;35761;TOOLONG invigorated . We had a really great time making it . Just have the most fun , that&amp;rsquo ; s our mission this entire year . Once we decided we&amp;rsquo ; re going to call it , we&amp;rsquo ; re like , &amp;lsquo ; Alright , we&amp;rsquo ; ve been having the best time , let&amp;rsquo ; s do that again for a year , &amp;rsquo ; so that&amp;rsquo ; s totally the mantra of everything we&amp;rsquo ; re doing this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Does it actually feel like the final chapter ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Yeah , totally . We couldn&amp;rsquo ; t be happier . We&amp;rsquo ; re really thankful for everything , all the friends we&amp;rsquo ; ve met is insane . Everyone in our lives is somehow related to this band . So we&amp;rsquo ; re not moving on past that , you can&amp;rsquo ; t . But as far as making music as Hollerado , yeah , that&amp;rsquo ; s run its course . It&amp;rsquo ; s totally fine . No one should freak out about that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Any regrets ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : We definitely made a lot of mistakes . When you&amp;rsquo ; re supposed to be making your third album &amp;mdash ; after you made a great first album and a second album that does really well , your third album is supposed to take you to the next level and you decide to make 111 songs and you spend two years making it , and they&amp;rsquo ; re songs that cover all styles of music , it&amp;rsquo ; s career suicide . As we learned . But we don&amp;rsquo ; t regret it at all . I would do it again . What an experience , what a way to learn about ourselves . It&amp;rsquo ; s collectively one of the projects that we are most proud of . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Are fans expressing their sadness about the demise of Hollerado ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Oh , of course , big time . We&amp;rsquo ; re trying to tell them , &amp;lsquo ; Dude , there&amp;rsquo ; s way more important things to be sad about . &amp;rsquo ; The records are still there , come to these shows and have a good time , and then get on with your life . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; lsaxberg@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;35788;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicOur Bluesfest daily picks : Taz and Shakey Graveshttp : **34;35940;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;35976;TOOLONG ... title= " Brandon Taz Niederauer. " alt= " Brandon Taz Niederauer. " data-portal-copyright= " HANDOUT " **28;36012;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1426405 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;i&gt;RBC Bluesfest returns to LeBreton Flats for 10 days of musical revelry from July 4 to 14 . We 'll be bringing you our top picks for every day of the festival . For Thursday , July 11 , check out Taz and Shakey Graves . &lt;/i&gt;&lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36042;TOOLONG ... title= " Brandon Taz Niederauer. " alt= " Brandon Taz Niederauer. " data-portal-copyright= " HANDOUT " **28;36078;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1426405 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Brandon ' Taz ' **25;36108;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;7:30-8:30 p.m . July 11 , Bluesville Stage&lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;If you have youngsters in your life who play guitar , take them to this show . Niederauer is a 15-year-old New Yorker who plays with the intensity of a Tasmanian devil , hence the nickname Taz . It all started when he was eight , saw the movie School of Rock , and knew what he wanted to do with his life . Just four years later , he had learned so much that he starred in the Broadway production of the film , and has since shared the stage with the likes of Derek Trucks , Buddy Guy , Lady Gaga , Gary Clark Jr . and many more of his musical heroes . &lt;/span&gt; Particularly sweet was the shout-out from John Mayer , who said he&amp;rsquo ; s the &amp;ldquo ; first young guitar player ( who ) is not a gimmick. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; -- Lynn Saxberg&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36135;TOOLONG ... title= " Shakey Graves . " alt= " Shakey Graves . " data-portal-copyright= " Giles Clement " **28;36171;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1426412 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Shakey Graves&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; July 11 , Bluesville Stage , 9 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fast becoming a festival fan-favourite , the Austin , Texas-born Alejandro Rose-Garcia has been kicking up plenty of dust on the festival circuit over the last 10 years as Shakey Graves . Whether he&amp;rsquo ; s equipped with only a battered old acoustic and a kick drum in a boot-stomping one-man band , or rocking his three-piece combo , Rose-Garcia is a born performer and gets people moving . A gifted storyteller , his latest album Can&amp;rsquo ; t Wake Up draws some of its lyrical inspiration from The Wizard of Oz and classic Disney cartoons . The Bluesfest gig is a full-band show . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; -- Aedan Helmer&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;36201;TOOLONG ... says OC Transpo driver reluctant to help harassed female passengers : ' He was so indifferent ' &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;36236;TOOLONG ...
