ArtsMusicThe NACO just toured in Europe to ' amazed ' audiences , elevating the orchestra to one of the best aroundhttp : **34;40191;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;40227;TOOLONG ... title= " James Ehnes performing at Stockholm 's Royal Chapel . " alt= " James Ehnes performing at Stockholm 's Royal Chapel . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;40263;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403128 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;40293;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; STOCKHOLM &amp;mdash ; In the hallowed opulence of the gilt-edged , marble-clad Royal Chapel in Stockholm&amp;rsquo ; s Royal Palace , James Ehnes performed a devilishly difficult solo piece on his violin , the music soaring to the peak of the centuries-old sanctuary . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As usual , he performed the piece &amp;mdash ; Eug&amp;egrave ; ne Ysa&amp;yuml ; e&amp;rsquo ; s Sonata No. 3 &amp;mdash ; without sheet music and his expression revealed nothing . He was intent , totally focused on the music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From my vantage point a few feet away in a front-row pew , I realized I was holding my breath as the musical suspense built . When he finished , there was an overwhelming sense of release . Was I going to cry ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I managed to hold it in . Instead , I recalled the words of one of my new orchestra friends at a bar in Paris a few days earlier . &amp;ldquo ; When you hear James play , you&amp;rsquo ; re going to want to throw your bra at him , &amp;rdquo ; she declared . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the moment of revelry , everyone laughed , but now I understood . The man is a musical genius , a rare talent whose playing connects with the deepest corners of one&amp;rsquo ; s soul . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;40329;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;40371;TOOLONG ... soloist : Fighting challenge after challenge , soprano Erin Wall rises to the **27;40406;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;40435;TOOLONG ... David DQ Lee wows the crowd in London with incredible , multi-octave **26;40470;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; I was not the only person to come to this conclusion during the National Arts Centre Orchestra&amp;rsquo ; s recent tour of Europe . Although Ehnes records and tours extensively and has won two Grammys , including this year&amp;rsquo ; s honour for the best classical instrumental solo , it seems many people in Europe had never heard of him , judging by the awestruck reaction of audiences after hearing him perform Mendelssohn&amp;rsquo ; s Violin Concerto with the orchestra . Whenever he played , jaws dropped and people rose to their feet shouting , &amp;ldquo ; Bravo . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; " We were very amazed , " said one Danish concertgoer , 28-year-old &lt;/span&gt; Mika Dahl , &amp;nbsp ; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;during the intermission in Copenhagen 's Danish Radio Konserthuset . " He put all of it in his head so he could just play ? That 's crazy . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Stockholm fans agreed . &amp;ldquo ; It was really a joy , this violinist , &amp;rdquo ; said Kerstin Nilsson , a season subscriber to the city&amp;rsquo ; s Konserthuset classical music series . &amp;ldquo ; That was amazing . It was marvellous . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40498;TOOLONG ... title= " James Ehnes performing at Stockholm 's Royal Chapel . " alt= " James Ehnes performing at Stockholm 's Royal Chapel . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;40534;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403128 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ehnes was one of several featured soloists during the orchestra&amp;rsquo ; s two-week journey , their guest appearances divided over seven concerts in five countries . It was the most ambitious tour of Alexander Shelley&amp;rsquo ; s four-year tenure as maestro of the Ottawa-based orchestra , and not something to be taken lightly . An orchestra has to be good to hold its own in the birthplace of classical music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After leading NACO to every region in Canada during the 2017 sesquicentennial celebrations , the idea of showing the musicians off in Europe for the NAC&amp;rsquo ; s 50th anniversary became a personal goal for Shelley , a 39-year-old Englishman who has conducted orchestras on five continents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The interesting part of the title &amp;lsquo ; national&amp;rsquo ; for any institution is that on the one hand , it implies the local responsibility , the responsibility to the country , &amp;rdquo ; he said between forkfuls of salad and sips of coffee during a pre-concert interview in his dressing room in Utrecht&amp;rsquo ; s TivoliVredenburg concert hall . &amp;ldquo ; But interestingly , it immediately also implies the representative , ambassadorial role of representing the country out in the world . We also take that role very seriously . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; *** &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Manitoba-born Ehnes was the crowd favourite on a tour expertly programmed to highlight world-class Canadian talent in performance and composition . For Ehnes , the experience never gets old . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You always hope to cultivate new audiences , &amp;rdquo ; he said in an interview in the Royal Chapel . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve played in all these places but every concert is an opportunity . One of the great joys of doing what we do is there&amp;rsquo ; s always somebody there who&amp;rsquo ; s there for the first time , or somebody who will be left thinking about the music in a new way . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Other musicians joke that &amp;ldquo ; Jimmy&amp;rdquo ; must have sold his soul or been implanted with some sort of computer chip to be able to play so fluidly , without breaking a sweat or cracking a smile . But no : it takes discipline and hard work to make it look so easy . He said his primary goal is to get out of the way of the music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In the rock world , there are people who perform in different ways to draw attention to different aspects of what they do , &amp;rdquo ; Ehnes said . &amp;ldquo ; But I think what we do with our music is to try to draw attention away from ourselves and draw more attention to the music being played . The performer plays a role of conduit more than a role of star . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No matter what he says , Ehnes is undoubtedly a star , considered by many to be among the best violinists in the world , perhaps even a rival to the great Pinchas Zukerman , NACO&amp;rsquo ; s previous music director and a superstar in the classical world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m happy to report Ehnes is also a humble , down-to-earth fellow &amp;mdash ; a husband and father of two young children &amp;mdash ; with a surprising penchant for roller coasters , of all things . On his downtime in Copenhagen , he headed straight to Tivoli Gardens . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s the most wonderful place , &amp;rdquo ; he said of the amusement park . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve played in Copenhagen a bunch over the years , and whenever possible , I try to amuse myself there . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40564;TOOLONG ... title= " Alexander Shelley ( R ) and Canadian violinist James Ehnes . The NAC orchestra on tour in Europe . " alt= " Alexander Shelley ( R ) and Canadian violinist James Ehnes . The NAC orchestra on tour in Europe . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;40600;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403119 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; An international orchestra tour does not come together overnight . In addition to the logistics of booking concerts and making travel arrangements , it&amp;rsquo ; s important to present a well-balanced evening . Shelley assembled five different programs , selecting both contemporary and classic material , and highlighting Canadian content . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To anchor the traditional end , there were two crowd-pleasers , Brahms Symphony No. 2 and Dvorak&amp;rsquo ; s Symphony No. 9 in E minor , Op. 95 , a.k.a . From The New World , both of which sound glorious in NACO&amp;rsquo ; s hands ( and which Shelley conducts without a score ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for the guest soloists , another budding superstar , the dazzling Calgary-born pianist Jan Lisiecki , was featured in the two United Kingdom concerts in London and Saffron Walden . Like Ehnes , he&amp;rsquo ; s a stupendously talented regular on the international circuit . Lesser known are the Korean-Canadian countertenor David D.Q. Lee and soprano Erin Wall , who both gained valuable exposure on the journey . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of particular significance was the inclusion of contemporary music by homegrown composers , a bold move for any ensemble , but one Shelley also considers a vital part of a national orchestra&amp;rsquo ; s role . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The amount of new music we&amp;rsquo ; re travelling with is unprecedented , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I can think of very few orchestras in the world that would do that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A glowing review of the Cadogan Hall concert in Chelsea called it &amp;ldquo ; one of the major highlights of the current London orchestral season , &amp;rdquo ; with special praise for the piece &amp;ldquo ; Golden slumbers kiss your eyes&amp;hellip ; &amp;rdquo ; by Serbian-Canadian composer Ana Sokolovic , in which Lee is the featured soloist . A classicalsource.com reviewer described her as a &amp;ldquo ; completely assured composer &amp;hellip ; never losing sight of what this compelling work is intended to convey to its listeners . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In all , works by six Canadian composers were featured on the tour , including the four-part , multimedia flagship piece , Life Reflected , an NAC commission , which had its European premiere in Paris and was also performed in Gothenburg , Sweden . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40630;TOOLONG ... title= " Life Reflected on tour in Europe . " alt= " Life Reflected on tour in Europe . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;40666;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403123 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; With the text translated into French surtitles , the challenging Life Reflected went over well at La Seine Musicale , a spacious new facility just outside the core of Paris , and even better at the Konserthuset in Gothenburg . There , with Swedish surtitles , it was performed on the final night of the Point Music Festival , a celebration of new music , and received a standing ovation. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s so special when a piece does jump the border and leaves Canada , whether it&amp;rsquo ; s elsewhere in North America or to Europe , &amp;rdquo ; said composer John Estacio , who wrote the music to I Lost My Talk , one of the four parts of Life Reflected . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Usually a piece is performed in , and lives in , the country that commissioned it , and doesn&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily go beyond that so it&amp;rsquo ; s a lovely moment when another country gets to hear your music , &amp;rdquo ; he added . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His majestic score augments an arrangement of the poem by the late Mi&amp;rsquo ; kmaw poet laureate Rita Joe that was sparked by her time in residential school , where students were forbidden from speaking their languages . The poem is accompanied by a film depicting a company of Indigenous dancers performing on the rocky shores of Georgian Bay , and an Indigenous actor , Monique Mojica , whose film appearance is mirrored by an appearance on stage . Her real-life presence underscores the resilience of Indigenous Peoples who survived Canada&amp;rsquo ; s residential-school system . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Life Reflected is produced and directed by Stratford hitmaker Donna Feore , who worked closely with Shelley as artistic director , and brought together artists from different generations , cultures and backgrounds . The piece also features segments on Nobel prize-winning author Alice Munro , astronaut Roberta Bondar and a particularly poignant section on B.C. teenager Amanda Todd , who took her own life after being bullied online . Amanda&amp;rsquo ; s mother , Carol Todd , was in the audience in Gothenburg . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40696;TOOLONG ... title= " The NACO on tour in Europe . " alt= " The NACO on tour in Europe . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;40732;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403130 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As someone born outside Canada , Shelley lacks the typical Canuck reluctance to toot one&amp;rsquo ; s own horn . He tells everyone that NACO is one of the finest orchestras in the world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If it was a hockey league or any other sports league , they&amp;rsquo ; d be one of the top teams in the world , no question about that , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; The challenge on this tour is to take it out of the local league and go into the international league . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Stockholm , London , Paris , Copenhagen , they have their own teams . And they&amp;rsquo ; re great , but what I want is for our Canadian national team to be out there in those centres showcasing the quality and the craftsmanship of the orchestra . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His enthusiasm is convincing , and the orchestra lived up to the billing . Their playing was consistently excellent throughout the tour , and the team spirit grew with every day on the road as the group hustled to catch trains , buses and flights . Fortunately there was only one day when it was necessary to travel and perform a concert the same evening . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It helps us to bond in a very different way , &amp;rdquo ; said Elaine Klimasko , a violinist who&amp;rsquo ; s been with the orchestra since its first year in 1969. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re really a team . I find that people really become so much more friendly . Most are out without their spouses and you find you just sit down with a colleague you might have only shared a couple of stages with , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;40762;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now 69 , she&amp;rsquo ; s been on almost every tour , starting with the 1973 excursion to Russia and Eastern Europe with then-conductor Mario Bernardi. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; These days , she travels with her husband , Ed Blais , who considers it a vacation . He helps with luggage and scouts out interesting sites to see and restaurants to try . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although her health is good and she feels she has more energy than many her age , Klimasko is beginning to consider retirement . She and Blais own a condo in Florida , and she&amp;rsquo ; s the artistic director of Ottawa Suzuki Strings , a music school co-founded by her sister . Teaching senior violin students has long been a passion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In fact , one night in Utrecht , Klimasko returned to the hotel after a post-concert outing to a local bar in order to FaceTime a student in Ottawa who wanted help preparing some music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She was also one of several orchestra musicians who lent their expertise during masterclasses with local students along the journey . Others visited schools to perform for , or with , youngsters . No fewer than 60 community engagement activities were on the schedule , including the noon-hour concert in the Stockholm&amp;rsquo ; s Royal Chapel that also featured Ehnes&amp;rsquo ; above-mentioned performance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40798;TOOLONG ... title= " Alexander Shelley ( L ) , his wife Zoe , and baby Sasha at the train station with NAC staffer Fletcher Gailey-Snell in between . " alt= " Alexander Shelley ( L ) , his wife Zoe , and baby Sasha at the train station with NAC staffer Fletcher Gailey-Snell in between . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;40834;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403125 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Klimasko wasn&amp;rsquo ; t the only musician to bring a spouse . Some even brought children , including Shelley and his wife , Zoe , who travelled with their nine-month-old Ottawa-born son , Sasha , an inquisitive , good-natured baby who loved bouncing with Dad and didn&amp;rsquo ; t mind his mum passing him around for others to snuggle . The only problem was the virus he transmitted to both parents , leading to Shelley&amp;rsquo ; s first cold in two years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another couple with kids in tow was violist Paul Casey and cellist Karen Casey , with Karen&amp;rsquo ; s mother along to help care for almost two-year-old Oak and her busy big brother , Fitz , who&amp;rsquo ; s almost four , when the parents were in rehearsal or on stage . A few dozen orchestra pals also helped entertain the pre-schoolers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For some of the musical travellers , the tour offered a chance to sample fine restaurants in each city . In their time off , they visited museums , went on walking tours and checked out the nightlife . Their discipline was evident , too , as many sequestered themselves in their hotel rooms to practice , the sounds of viola or trumpet or flute filling the hallways. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40864;TOOLONG ... title= " NAC Orchestra viola player Paul Casey and his wife , cellist Karen Casey , travelled with both of their children on the orchestra 's 50th anniversary tour of Europe . " alt= " NAC Orchestra viola player Paul Casey and his wife , cellist Karen Casey , travelled with both of their children on the orchestra 's 50th anniversary tour of Europe . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;40900;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403124 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The difficulties were infrequent . In London , two musicians were pickpocketed in separate incidents , an early-tour warning for everyone to secure their valuables . One musician suffered bruises after taking a tumble on the uneven cobblestone streets , while another lost a shoe in the scramble to get from concert to reception in Paris . An irreplaceable instrument was inadvertently left behind in Saffron Waldon , an oversight that required crew members to find a vehicle and drive back to the venue to retrieve it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bonding opportunities , on the other hand , were abundant . One of the most memorable moments happened during a whirlwind stop in Copenhagen , where the island hotel was conveniently located next to an outdoor ocean-water pool , the bottom secured with netting to separate swimmers from Baltic Sea life . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That evening&amp;rsquo ; s fun started with a plentiful supply of post-concert beer on tap backstage , compliments of the Danish Symphony . It continued at a hipster cocktail bar , and culminated with an invigorating 2 a.m. dip in the Baltic Sea . Among the handful of musicians who plunged into the frigid ocean was cellist Julia MacLaine , who was celebrating her 37th birthday . &amp;ldquo ; Best . Birthday . Ever , &amp;rdquo ; she declared with a smile . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40930;TOOLONG ... title= " Cellist Julia MacLaine ( left , in blue ) celebrated her 37th birthday in Copenhagen on May 21 . After a 2 a.m. dip in the Baltic Sea , she declared it the ' best birthday ever . ' " alt= " Cellist Julia MacLaine ( left , in blue ) celebrated her 37th birthday in Copenhagen on May 21 . After a 2 a.m. dip in the Baltic Sea , she declared it the ' best birthday ever . ' " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;40966;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403122 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Airfare , accommodations and ground transportation for more than 100 people doesn&amp;rsquo ; t come cheaply , and there was no extra funding from the federal government to pay for the tour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Instead , the bulk of the journey was funded by donors , about 25 of whom followed the orchestra on their own NAC staff-organized tour , which involved first-class travel , five-star hotels and formal receptions with Canada&amp;rsquo ; s dignitaries in each of the five countries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The group included the tour&amp;rsquo ; s main benefactors , Janice and Earl O&amp;rsquo ; Borne , as well as NAC Foundation patrons Dasha Shenkman and Elinor Gill Ratcliffe , who described herself as an &amp;ldquo ; orchestra groupie. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The reason why people donate to orchestras is we like the music , &amp;rdquo ; said the 77-year-old philanthropist from Newfoundland and Labrador during a pre-concert interview in the lobby of Copenhagen&amp;rsquo ; s elegant Angleterre Hotel . &amp;ldquo ; If you don&amp;rsquo ; t have the money to pay for the conductor , in this case a magnificent conductor , and to keep good musicians , you don&amp;rsquo ; t get any music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So you do it for the music , you do it for your country . We are putting Canada , including Indigenous pieces , on the world stage . Personally I find that very exciting as a Canadian and as a human being , and I think this is a wonderful way to connect with people . For me , the arts is that little extra that gives us humanity , gives us the joy of life . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; NAC CEO Christopher Deacon , who was also on the tour , considered it one of the most successful NACO tours in its 50-year history . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What was especially meaningful to me was the fact that we were able to bring the music of six Canadian composers , and present them in major European capitals , &amp;rdquo ; he said in Stockholm , also pointing to the high level of artistry displayed by the soloists . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This really aligns with one of our strategic priorities , to help put Canada&amp;rsquo ; s artistic expression on the world stage . I think the European public , which is quite discerning , was suitably impressed with the very high standard of excellence . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As were we all . Come to think of it , I could be an orchestra groupie now , too &amp;mdash ; although I doubt I&amp;rsquo ; ll be tossing my undergarments at Jimmy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; lsaxberg@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;40996;TOOLONG ... of the National Arts Centre 's opening week in 1969&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;41031;TOOLONG ... columnist Randall Denley speaks about his Ottawa-based murder mystery&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;41066;TOOLONG ... has to rebuild her whole life : ' $19 million lawsuit filed on behalf of woman who lost legs in OC Transpo crash&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Lynn SaxbergThu , 30 May 2019 15:37:47 **45;41101;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal NewsMusicNAC soloist : Fighting challenge after challenge , soprano Erin Wall rises to the momenthttp : **34;41148;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;41184;TOOLONG ... title= " Canadian soprano Erin Wall was the featured soloist in several concerts with the NAC Orchestra on its European tour , including Paris , Utrecht , Copenhagen , Stockholm and Gothenburg . " alt= " Canadian soprano Erin Wall was the featured soloist in several concerts with the NAC Orchestra on its European tour , including Paris , Utrecht , Copenhagen , Stockholm and Gothenburg . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;41220;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403108 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Erin Wall grew up with firsthand knowledge of the challenges of a career in classical music . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Canadian soprano is the daughter of a French horn player and a cellist who both played in the Vancouver Symphony Orchestra . During her childhood , the symphony&amp;rsquo ; s financial situation was a regular cause for concern in her family . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve lived that , &amp;rdquo ; the 43-year-old singer said during a candid and wide-ranging interview in Utrecht , Netherlands . &amp;ldquo ; Twice in my childhood there was either a lockout or strike , or bankruptcy problems . It was often a great cloud hanging over us. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;41250;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;41292;TOOLONG ... NACO just toured in Europe to ' amazed ' audiences , elevating the orchestra to one of the best **27;41327;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;41356;TOOLONG ... years of magic : CEO Christopher Deacon on the NAC 's **32;41391;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;41425;TOOLONG ... David DQ Lee wows the crowd in London with incredible , multi-octave **26;41460;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Still , she found her way to a similar , arguably even more challenging career as an opera singer , a gig that doesn&amp;rsquo ; t usually offer the companionship of an entire orchestra and requires one to be comfortable performing demanding material in the spotlight . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Naturally , Wall was honoured to be invited along as a featured soloist on the NAC Orchestra&amp;rsquo ; s recent tour of Europe , even though it meant mastering some extremely difficult pieces . It was her fourth tour with an orchestra , but the first with an ensemble from Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her voluptuous voice provides the ethereal upper thread in Dear Life , the Alice Munro-inspired segment of the multi-media new work , Life Reflected , which was performed in Paris and Gothenburg , Sweden . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For one Paris concertgoer , Wall&amp;rsquo ; s performance was the evening&amp;rsquo ; s standout moment . &amp;ldquo ; My God , she&amp;rsquo ; s incredible , &amp;rdquo ; said the Canadian ex-pat , who&amp;rsquo ; s been living in the City of Light for more than a decade . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41488;TOOLONG ... title= " Canadian soprano Erin Wall was the featured soloist in several concerts with the NAC Orchestra on its European tour , including Paris , Utrecht , Copenhagen , Stockholm and Gothenburg . " alt= " Canadian soprano Erin Wall was the featured soloist in several concerts with the NAC Orchestra on its European tour , including Paris , Utrecht , Copenhagen , Stockholm and Gothenburg . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;41524;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403111 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In four cities on the tour , Wall also sang The Lonely Child , a haunting 1980 work for soprano and orchestra by Canadian composer Claude Vivier . It&amp;rsquo ; s considered the most personal and spiritual composition by the ill-fated character , who was abandoned by his parents as a baby , made no secret of the fact that he was gay and died before his time , murdered in Paris in 1983 , a month before his 35th birthday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some audiences didn&amp;rsquo ; t know what to make of the piece , which is based on non-Western musical forms and calls for the singer , who&amp;rsquo ; s acting as a mother reassuring her lonely child , to touch her fingers to her lips to create musical noises , as if speaking gibberish to a child . It&amp;rsquo ; s especially intense for the singer because there are no breaks and she has to sing in an ever-escalating pitch . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But as is often the case with love-it-or-hate-it nature of contemporary classical music , it was an exciting discovery for many adventurous listeners . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Claude Vivier is completely new for us , &amp;rdquo ; said Adriaan Vanes , a Dutch music fan who attended the Utrecht concert with his wife and friends visiting from London . &amp;ldquo ; To not only hear his very tragic life story but ( to also hear ) his music , it&amp;rsquo ; s compelling . It&amp;rsquo ; s new . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His London friend concurred . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a very interesting and important experience to hear new voices and in this case , to hear the writing and the music of somebody who had , on the face of it , quite a difficult life , &amp;rdquo ; said James Welsh . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re extremely happy to have this opportunity . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , audiences had no idea of the additional challenges Wall faced in getting to those European stages . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After she was invited on tour and given the repertoire , she was diagnosed with breast cancer . With an arts-employed husband and two young children at home in the Toronto area , she continued to work during her treatment . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Six rounds of chemo , two surgeries and a few months later , Wall was still determined to complete the tour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I knew taking on the Vivier would be a really big challenge , &amp;rdquo ; she said , &amp;ldquo ; but because I had been involved in Dear Life from its inception , I wanted to be a part of that . It&amp;rsquo ; s fun to be part of something new . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There are so many orchestras going , &amp;lsquo ; How can we reinvent this to get people to come , to make it modern and relevant and interesting ? &amp;rsquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve never done anything like Life Reflected before , and I think it works . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;LISTEN TO THE DOWN TO BUSINESS PODCAST&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe src= " **40;41554;TOOLONG ... Lynn SaxbergThu , 30 May 2019 15:36:51 **45;41596;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicCanada 's David DQ Lee wows the crowd in London with incredible , multi-octave voicehttp : **35;41643;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;41680;TOOLONG ... title= " David DQ Lee performing with NACO. " alt= " David DQ Lee performing with NACO. " data-portal-copyright= " PROVIDED " **28;41716;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403104 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s unlikely that British audiences have ever seen a singer quite like David DQ Lee . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Korean-Canadian countertenor , whose full name is David Dong-Qyu Lee , is the featured soloist in Ana Sokovic&amp;rsquo ; s Juno-winning piece of contemporary classical music , Golden slumbers kiss your eyes&amp;hellip ; , performed by the NAC Orchestra as part of its program at Cadogan Hall in London , England . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 20-minute composition featured a choir &amp;mdash ; on this night , London Voices &amp;mdash ; with a spotlight role written specifically to show off Lee&amp;rsquo ; s incredible , multi-octave voice . Although billed as a countertenor , meaning he&amp;rsquo ; s a singer with a masterful command of the high end of the male range , he&amp;rsquo ; s also capable of resoundingly deep bass notes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Sokovic&amp;rsquo ; s intricate and moving work , he gets the rare chance to show off both ends of his vocal spectrum , and he nailed it , demonstrating incredible control and impeccable phrasing . One seasoned British fan in the audience was so impressed he approached an NAC staff member to share his enthusiasm and find out Lee&amp;rsquo ; s name . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For Lee , who&amp;rsquo ; s 41 , the response was encouraging . It&amp;rsquo ; s been almost a decade since he last performed in London , and he knows that British music fans tend to prefer a different style of countertenor . Plus , he added , they&amp;rsquo ; re not used to diversity in classical singing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This is their culture , &amp;rdquo ; he said , referring to classical music in general during an interview in his dressing room after the concert . &amp;ldquo ; It can be very racist . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41746;TOOLONG ... title= " The NAC orchestra on tour in Europe with countertenor David D.Q. Lee performing . " alt= " The NAC orchestra on tour in Europe with countertenor David D.Q. Lee performing . " data-portal-copyright= " CATTROLL FRED " **28;41782;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403112 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The English style of countertenor is closer to a boy soprano , Lee explained , describing it &amp;ldquo ; very angelic and innocent . &amp;rdquo ; His style is more North American , robust and full-throated. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; English countertenors are reigning and they prefer those countertenors , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s really hard to be back here and sell as an operatic countertenor . They definitely have a preference . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Born in South Korea , Lee had early training in classic vocal technique as a boy soprano in a prestigious Korean children&amp;rsquo ; s choir . When his family moved to Vancouver , he joined a boys&amp;rsquo ; choir . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then my voice broke and I had this low voice as a male , but I still had the muscle memory so I could actually go into the higher register , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I still had no idea what a countertenor was until my choir director in high school told me , &amp;lsquo ; You are a countertenor. &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He discovered the movie , Fattadelli , about a singer who had been castrated to maintain his high-pitched voice . Lee could hit the same notes , and was thrilled to discover there was a repertoire for the range . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He found himself auditioning for both bass and countertenor training , eventually landing with Canadian mezzo-soprano Phyllis Mailing , the first wife of famed composer R. Murray Schaffer , and a teacher Lee considers his mentor. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; She took me in and taught me a lot of vocal technique and how to maintain my voice better , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41812;TOOLONG ... title= " David DQ Lee performing with NACO. " alt= " David DQ Lee performing with NACO. " data-portal-copyright= " PROVIDED " **28;41848;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1403104 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So there I was . At first no one knew what to do with me so I was training as a bass . It was like &amp;lsquo ; Ok , this repertoire has a bass but it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t excite me , &amp;rsquo ; so I switched back full time to counter tenor . And then I was auditioning and doing competitions and the results were really good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s how I began as a countertenor. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;LISTEN TO THE 10/3 PODCAST&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe src= " **40;41878;TOOLONG ... " alt= " Robert Plant and the Sensational Space Shifters closes out the 2019 CityFolk festival at Lansdowne Park in September . " data-portal-copyright= " Photo by Joel Ryan/Invision/AP " **28;42037;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1399094 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Former Led Zeppelin frontman Robert Plant , Americana singer/songwriter Lucinda Williams and soul singer Leon Bridges are among the headliners for Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s CityFolk festival at Lansdowne Park this September . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The lineup , announced Tuesday , also features Canadian folkies Donovan Woods and Tim Baker and veteran rockers Our Lady Peace during the four-day festival that runs Sept. 12-15. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Larkin Poe &amp;mdash ; sisters Rebecca and Megan Lovelle of Atlanta &amp;mdash ; will add some roots rock to the mix while the&amp;nbsp ; B.C.-based Snotty Nose Rez Kids will bring their Juno-nominated Indigenous hip-hop flavour to the festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Since moving to Lansdowne Park in 2015 , CityFolk has become an important staple for music fans to celebrate the onset of fall , &amp;rdquo ; CityFolk&amp;rsquo ; s executive and artistic director Mark Monahan said in a release . &amp;ldquo ; The festival has established an end-of-summer tradition&amp;mdash ; cottages are closed , kids are back in school &amp;mdash ; and CityFolk gives people one last chance to enjoy a great outdoor music experience . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Williams , touring on the 20th anniversary of the release of her Grammy-winning album Car Wheels on a Gravel Road , headlines the festival&amp;rsquo ; s opening night on Thursday , Sept. 12. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Summersault show of Our Lady Peace , Live and Bush , take the stage on Sept. 13 ; Bridges headlines Sept. 14 , while Robert Plant and the Sensational Space Shifters close out the festival on Sunday , Sept. 15. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The popular Marvest free shows by local acts will again be hosted in various venues around the festival site . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Other confirmed CityFolk performers include The Strumbellas , Rich Aucoin , Saul Williams , The Once , Dwayne Gretzky , Harrow Fair , LeE HARVeY OsMOND and the White Buffalo . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; An early , pre-sale offer of discount tickets begins Thursday , May 23 at 10 a.m. , with regular ticket sales beginning Friday , May 24 at 10 a.m . More details on performers and ticket sales is available at&amp;nbsp ; &lt;a href= " http : **26;42067;TOOLONG " &gt;www ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42095;TOOLONG ... title= " Lucinda Williams " alt= " Lucinda Williams " data-portal-copyright= " JASON HALSTEAD/SUN MEDIA " **28;42131;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1399095 " /&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;42161;TOOLONG ... brothers run for their lost , identical twins&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;42196;TOOLONG ... educators offered padded shirts , arm and shin guards to protect themselves from violent students&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;42231;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicGG 's performing arts awards : Epic Canadiana , surprise guests Jann Arden , Emily Haines , TPOHhttp : **36;42313;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;42351;TOOLONG ... title= " Actress Sandra Oh , recipient of the National Arts Centre Award , arrives on the red carpet at the Governor General 's Performing Arts Awards at the National Arts Centre in Ottawa on Saturday , April 26 , 2019. " alt= " Actress Sandra Oh , recipient of the National Arts Centre Award , arrives on the red carpet at the Governor General 's Performing Arts Awards at the National Arts Centre in Ottawa on Saturday , April 26 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Patrick Doyle " **28;42387;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1387688 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Pop songstress Jann Arden , Metric singer Emily Haines and rockers The Pursuit of Happiness were among the performers who showed up to the National Arts Centre to surprise the winners of the Governor General 's Performing Arts Awards on Saturday . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Haines may have been the biggest surprise because she was supposed to be at TD Place getting ready to rock out with Metric . Instead , she sang a smouldering version of a song from the soundtrack to Killing Eve , the hit BBC America show that features Ottawa native Sandra Oh , who was one of the laureates being feted at the gala . Oh received the National Arts Centre award in recognition of a stellar performance year . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Wearing a dazzling catsuit , Haines gave a sultry take on the tune , Killer Shangri-Lah , with the the lush sounds of the NAC Orchestra making it an early-evening highlight . After demonstrating her love for Oh , who appeared to be enjoying every moment from the laureates ' row in the upper mezzanine , Haines quickly departed for her own concert . ( " Do n't take Elgin , " someone shouted after her. ) &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Near the end of the night , Arden did n't sing but spoke fondly of her laureate pal , writer/comedian Rick Mercer , and unveiled his surprise musical tribute guest . " Tonight we have put together a cross-country , Canada rock ' n ' roll band of epic proportions , " she declared , introducing the members of TPOH , who were joined by the NAC Orchestra and a dance troupe of students from Earl of March Secondary School . They performed a rousing rendition of the old Trooper nugget , Raise a Little Hell , that was , indeed , an epic moment of Canadiana. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The tribute to each laureate consisted of a few words from friends or colleagues , as well as a musical segment and a film short produced by the National Film Board . The film about Oh offered a compelling perspective of her influence on Asian representation in showbiz , while actor Colm Feore 's was a funny and surreal riff on what he might do in the afterlife ( because he 's done every type of acting gig in his real life ) . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The Mercer film was hilarious too , featuring a woke mom trying to inspire social consciousness in her son by shopping for Mercer 's line of Take Action figures . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;While there is no obligation for the laureates to actually perform , the only one to take advantage of the opportunity was pianist Louise Bessette , who was joined by the orchestra on an arrangement of Fur Louise . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Prima ballerina Karen Kain and protege Robert Binet , were part of the tribute to dance laureate Mavis Staines , a ballet educator . A modern spin on a pas de deux , choreographed by Binet , was beautifully performed by Leo Hepler and Genevieve Penn Nabity to music composed by Owen Pallett. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The film inspired by theatre laureate Lorraine Pintal was based on a 2016 play she directed , written by by Bertolt Brecht . " Lorraine is like a fire , " an actress intones , in French . " She fans the embers of passion . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Passion was also a theme of the tribute to Noel Spinelli , the sole laureate who did n't make it to the show , apparently due to health concerns . A music lover and business owner in Lachine , Spinelli received this year 's award for voluntarism . His tribute included a stunning version of a piece from Madama Butterfly , performed by soprano Caroline Bleau. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The night was ably hosted in both official languages by Heather Hiscox , who modeled several gorgeous dresses and also led the crowd in a round of " Happy Birthday " to the NAC on the occasion of its 50th anniversary , with help from singer Kim Richardson . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Of course , the Governor General , Julie Payette , was there , too , to deliver a rambling speech that tried to marry humour , math and the arts . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;42417;TOOLONG " **36;42448;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;42486;TOOLONG ... areas under threat as Ottawa River floodwaters to exceed record levels of 2017&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;42522;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicQueen C ? line to perform in Ottawa this fallhttp : **36;42862;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;42900;TOOLONG ... title= " C ? line Dion will be performing in Ottawa this fall . " alt= " C ? line Dion will be performing in Ottawa this fall . " data-portal-copyright= " Richard Shotwell " **28;42936;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1376087 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Queen of Pop will be popping into Ottawa this fall . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was announced Wednesday that C&amp;eacute ; line Dion will perform at Richcraft Live at the Canadian Tire Centre on Wednesday , Oct. 16 as part of her Courage World Tour . It is Dion&amp;rsquo ; s first full North American tour in over a decade . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a career spanning more than 35 years , Dion has sold close to a quarter-billion albums , and has won five Grammy Awards , two Academy Awards , seven American Music Awards and 20 Juno Awards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her residency at The Colosseum at Caesars Palace in Las Vegas has seen her perform more than 1,100 shows to more than 4.5 million fans . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tickets for her Ottawa show go on sale on Friday , April 12 at 10 a.m. , and are available at www.Ticketmaster.ca , **26;42966;TOOLONG , in person at The Sens Store at Carlingwood Mall , Place d&amp;rsquo ; Orl&amp;eacute ; ans and in the ByWard Market or at the Canadian Tire Centre box office . The Canadian Tire Centre box office does not sell tickets in-person on the first day of sales for this show . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;42994;TOOLONG ... and plaintiffs still in discussions over concussion-related class-action lawsuit&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;43030;TOOLONG ... days taken by teachers and educators at Ottawa public school board continue to climb&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;43065;TOOLONG ... hard to recall which hits are which , ' Coleman testifies at Boyle trial&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Bruce DeachmanWed , 03 Apr 2019 23:28:45 **45;43101;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicFamily looks for answers after popular musician struck down by blood clothttp : **36;43148;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;43186;TOOLONG ... title= " Janet Di Giacomo 's son Daniel , 31 , died suddenly in late February of a pulmonary embolism several weeks after orthopaedic surgery . Julie Oliver " alt= " Janet Di Giacomo 's son Daniel , 31 , died suddenly in late February of a pulmonary embolism several weeks after orthopaedic surgery . Julie Oliver " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;43222;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1374201 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Daniel Di Giacomo died in the early morning of Feb. 21 , struggling for breath on the kitchen floor as his incredulous father looked on . He was 31 years old . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The bassist for the up-and-coming country band the River Town Saints , Di Giacomo was a fun-loving bear of a man who had suffered a broken lower leg and dislocated ankle about seven weeks earlier . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His family believes the break prompted a fatal chain of events . They&amp;rsquo ; re looking for answers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; No one should got through what we have been going through , &amp;rdquo ; said Di Giacomo&amp;rsquo ; s mother , Janet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The cause of death is believed to be a thrombosis , or blood clot , that had traveLled to his lung , causing a pulmonary embolism . Di Giacomo&amp;rsquo ; s family says that if they had known a blood clot was a potentially fatal risk , they would have asked more questions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Six days before he died , he had clear symptoms of a blood clot , but we didn&amp;rsquo ; t know what it was , &amp;rdquo ; said his sister , Cristina. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Di Giacomo&amp;rsquo ; s father , Mike , says an embolism wasn&amp;rsquo ; t even on his radar . &amp;ldquo ; We didn&amp;rsquo ; t know what the warning signs were . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Janet said her attempts to get answers form Queensway Carleton Hospital about whether her son should have been sent home after his surgery with a list of warning signs and a prescription for blood thinners have been unsuccessful . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The hospital&amp;rsquo ; s chief of staff , Dr. Sanjay Acharya , wrote to Janet in an email dated March 22 that he has directed the department chief to review current practice relating to her concerns . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have already asked our risk managers to review the consents and how/if they need to be updated , &amp;rdquo ; Acharya wrote . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This is part of our internal review and what we would do after any critical incident . It would be our normal practice to invite you and your family to participate in any way you feel appropriate when we have gathered the basic information we need to make analysis . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Di Giacomo broke his leg just before noon Dec. 31 while delivering a television on an icy day . While he spent the summers touring with the River Town Saints , Di Giacomo worked a day job in the winter months . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He went first to the Almonte General Hospital and was transferred to Queensway Carleton . It took him 12 hours to see specialist . As was his way , he never breathed a word of impatience , said Janet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Di Giacomo could not be scheduled for surgery the next day and went home . He had day surgery on Jan. 3 , and was sent home with seven pages of instructions , including two pages for using crutches , a prescription for painkillers and a knee walker , a scooter-like device that allowed him to get around without putting weight on his ankle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He left the instructions on the coffee table of his west-end home , but Janet read every one of them . There was no mention of thrombosis or the risks around it or the suggestion that the patient should follow an exercise regime , she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The only mention was about wiggling his toes . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Daniel was the fourth of five children born to Janet and Mike . The family is musical . Janet played the guitar and piano and taught the boys to play . Di Giamcomo joined his first band at Bell High School , and was self-taught on the bass . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He loved being the centre of attention . Not in an offensive way . If you were in a room with Dan , he was the loudest guy and everyone would be laughing , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;nbsp ; said bandmate Jeremy Bortot. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43252;TOOLONG ... title= " Dan Di Giacomo was the bassist with the Ottawa band the River Town Saints . Photo by Sean Sisk Photography " alt= " Dan Di Giacomo was the bassist with the Ottawa band the River Town Saints . Photo by Sean Sisk Photography " data-portal-copyright= " SEAN SISK " **28;43288;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1374198 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Di Giamoco was also a deeply religious person and had studied at Bible college and spent two and a half years at a Los Angeles mission for the homeless called the Dream Center . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Dan had really strong beliefs . What people found super-cool about him was that he was pragmatic about it , &amp;rdquo ; says Bertot. &amp;ldquo ; He knew his theology , but he lived his life according to his beliefs . He was a good guy to other people . He was just kind . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The band criss-crossed the country in a 1988 Chevy van nicknamed Ron Burgundy and opened for a better-known acts . Di Giacomo&amp;rsquo ; s bandmates joked that he got his own room on tour because he snored like a grizzly bear sucking on a shop vac . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Saints played at every major country music festival in Eastern Canada and toured with country music stars Kip Moore and Dean Brody . The Saints&amp;rsquo ; singles Bonfire and Cherry Bomb got significant radio play and band was nominated for the Canadian Country Music Association award in the rising star category in 2016. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43318;TOOLONG ... title= " Chris McComb , Dan Di Giacomo , Jeremy Bortot of the River Town Saints.Photo by Sean Sisk Photography " alt= " Chris McComb , Dan Di Giacomo , Jeremy Bortot of the River Town Saints.Photo by Sean Sisk Photography " data-portal-copyright= " Sean Sisk " **28;43354;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1374199 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s hard to make a living from music . You have to work so hard . If you&amp;rsquo ; re just doing it for the money , you would be disappointed , &amp;rdquo ; said Bortot. &amp;ldquo ; It can&amp;rsquo ; t be about the destination ; it has to be about the ride . And it was such a great ride . If you saw Dan on stage , you would see someone who loved what he did . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the second week of February , Di Giacomo&amp;rsquo ; s recovery appeared to be slow . The band and its lead singer had parted ways last fall and was auditioning new singers in Bortot&amp;rsquo ; s basement on Feb. 14 , 15 and 16. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Di Giacomo still couldn&amp;rsquo ; t put any weight on his ankle . He had to sit while he played bass . He complained&amp;nbsp ; of fatigue , nausea and dizziness . When he returned home , it took 20 minutes&amp;nbsp ; to get him into the house . He later texted his friends that he still didn&amp;rsquo ; t feel back to normal and asked if future auditions could be spaced out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He was exhausted . We just thought he was healing . He couldn&amp;rsquo ; t do that much , &amp;rdquo ; says Janet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Sunday before he died , Di Giacomo gave his father a scare when he complained of chest pains . Mike gave him an aspirin and the pain subsided . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Di Giacomo told his parents he thought he over-exerted himself . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He wasn&amp;rsquo ; t the kind who abused the medical system , &amp;rdquo ; said Janet . &amp;ldquo ; A couple of people urged him to go to the hospital , but he probably didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to be packed up and sent home again . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the night of Feb. 20 , Di Giacomo spoke to his mother , who was in Pennsylvania , and spent his last few hours texting friends . It appears he got up for a glass of water in the early morning of Feb. 21. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His father woke up shortly after 2 a.m. and heard Di Giacomo calling for help . Mike found his son sitting on the kitchen floor , struggling for breath and semi-coherent . Mike called 911. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One paramedic team came and called for backup . Di Giacomo was hooked up to a monitor . His father , sitting on the stairs across the hall , said he saw his son&amp;rsquo ; s heartbeat flatline. &amp;nbsp ; Paramedics continued to administer CPR and rushed Di Gimacomo to the hospital with Mike following . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When Mike saw the doctor&amp;rsquo ; s face , he knew his son was dead . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shortly after , Mike spoke to a coroner who asked a few questions , including whether Daniel was taking blood thinners . Di Giacomo didn&amp;rsquo ; t hide anything from his family , and as far as Mike knew , his son wasn&amp;rsquo ; t taking blood thinners . That question got the wheels turning for Mike . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That begged the question &amp;mdash ; if it was a known risk , why was he not prescribed blood thinners ? My son just died . I was hearing a suggestion that it didn&amp;rsquo ; t have to happen , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; If the risk was there , why didn&amp;rsquo ; t we know about it ? That&amp;rsquo ; s what we want to know . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; March was Deep Vein Thrombosis Awareness Month . According to a survey commissioned by Thrombosis Canada last fall , only 43 per cent of Canadians know about the condition and only 25 per cent are concerned about it even though it can kill quickly . Vein blood blots affect about 100,000 Canadians and cause 10,000 deaths . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thrombosis Canada launched a campaign last month about the most common symptoms , which include chest pain , being lightheaded and out of breath , leg tenderness and leg swelling . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A spokeswoman for the Queensway Carleton said the hospital can&amp;rsquo ; t comment on the specifics of Di Giacomo&amp;rsquo ; s case , but its standard processes include discussing potential side effects , like blood clots or infections . Patients deemed to be at a higher risk are supported and treated on an individual basis . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For a patient who has had ankle surgery , the post-operative kit would include an information sheet indicating that &amp;ldquo ; some pain is normal for the first seven to 10 days , &amp;rdquo ; and urging the patient to call his or her surgeon or go to the nearest emergency department if there is severe pain not relieved with pain medication or if there is pain for more than seven to 10 days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The standard information package includes things to watch for , like pain , swelling , and changes in colour , said the spokeswoman . &amp;ldquo ; Some of these symptoms may indicate a blood clot or other issue . But the general package does not go into more details . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Blood clots are not a standard medical concern for ankle surgery , she said . &amp;ldquo ; Blood clots can be a general concern , depending on the type of surgery . Our largest at-risk surgery population is our total joint replacement patients &amp;mdash ; given the significant mobility challenges . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Janet believes that the risk of a post-operative embolism should have been made clear by the hospital , and it should have been in the handout package , along with instructions for an exercise regimen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I want to know if patients are getting this information . And if not , why ? Never in our worst nightmares did we know that it could be a fatal risk . We didn&amp;rsquo ; t even question it . We would have acted very differently , &amp;rdquo ; said Janet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s just another paragraph or two . Let people know , &amp;rdquo ; said Mike . &amp;ldquo ; Daniel was taken away at 31 years of age . It&amp;rsquo ; s not the parents that are supposed to bury their children . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;43384;TOOLONG ... jailed 30 days in 2010 for failing to care for elderly mother has conviction quashed&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;43420;TOOLONG ... lawsuit against OCH alleges reprisal for sympathizing with sexual harassment complainants&lt;/a&gt; &lt;strong&gt; &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;43456;TOOLONG ... are being left behind ' -- Families rally for national autism strategy&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Joanne LauciusMon , 01 Apr 2019 11:35:50 **45;43492;TOOLONG ... NewsMusicNew local band Psychedelic Sundaes to debut with groovy tunes , liquid light showhttp : **36;43539;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;43577;TOOLONG ... title= " Psychedelic Sundaes. " alt= " Psychedelic Sundaes. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43613;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1373011 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;A new Ottawa band that 's been rehearsing for two years will play their debut gig at Irene 's Pub on Saturday , with a 60s-inspired liquid light show designed and executed by the veteran Ottawa artist Arthur II . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The band calls itself Psychedelic Sundaes , and it 's led by guitarist Doug Gouthro , formerly of the esteemed Ottawa country-rock outfit The John Henrys . He 's joined by John Henrys ' drummer , Geoff Ward , bassist Ian Halman and multi-instrumentalist Ken Kanwisher on keyboards. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Their mission is to cover the spectrum of psychedelia , from the 1960s classics by Jefferson Airplane and The Grateful Dead to more contemporary fare by bands like The Flaming Lips . The band name was adapted from a fondly remembered Sunday-night radio program on a Toronto radio station . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " That 's the stuff I grew up on , " Gouthro said in an interview , referring to the early material . " I 'm an ' 80s kid but I was n't listening to that . I was listening to the old stuff . I just decided one day , ' I want to do that . Let 's do it . ' And those guys were on board . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;One of their first orders of business was to stick to the repertoire . In other words , no jamming . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43643;TOOLONG ... title= " Psychedelic Sundaes. " alt= " Psychedelic Sundaes. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43679;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1373009 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " I made it pretty clear , " Gouthro said . " Let 's not jam . Let 's get this first and then think about the other stuff . I just want to have the show together . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Although the rest of the band is itching to tackle some original material , Gouthro wants to establish the musical style and build a solid foundation first . The music is fairly complicated , he noted , requiring an array of sound-effect pedals to reproduce the guitar parts . He carries his pedals in two suitcases . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;As for the light show , it 's been in development as long as the music . Arthur II , who legally changed his surname to the Roman numeral decades ago , has dusted off a vintage overhead projector , and rounded up glass clock faces , liquid dyes and oils to create undulating puddles of coloured light that will be projected onto the band members as they perform . The old-school approach recalls the visual enhancement that was a hallmark of the psychedelic era , improvised on the spot by the lighting artist . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Said Gouthro : " He 's going to create stuff you just would n't see anywhere these days . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; ( The gig takes place Saturday at Irene&amp;rsquo ; s Pub , 885 Bank St. , starting around 9 p.m. with an opening set by The Loozin Brothers . Tickets are $16.50 advance at eventbrite.ca or $20 at the door. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; lsaxberg@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;43709;TOOLONG ... adds alt-J to opening night lineup&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;43744;TOOLONG ... gripe about failing Hunt Club/Riverside intersection , but approve major development anyway&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;43779;TOOLONG ... ones , Ottawa music community in mourning after fatal overdoses claim three lives&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Lynn SaxbergThu , 28 Mar 2019 20:18:54 **45;43814;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicChamberfest to f ? te Beethoven , a classic Beatles album and much more this yearhttp : **36;43861;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;43899;TOOLONG ... title= " Pianist Hinrich Alpers will perform 15 of Beethoven 's piano sonatas at this year 's Chamberfest. " alt= " Pianist Hinrich Alpers will perform 15 of Beethoven 's piano sonatas at this year 's Chamberfest. " data-portal-copyright= " HANDOUT " **28;43935;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 283620 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chamberfest this summer will mark a slew of anniversaries , from the 250th anniversary of Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s birth to the 50th anniversary of the Beatles&amp;rsquo ; Abbey Road album to the 25th anniversary of the festival itself . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among the event&amp;rsquo ; s 70 or so eclectic concerts released Tuesday are performances that also shine a light on the music of two lesser-known female composers , Barbara Strozzi and Clara Schumann , who will mark anniversaries in 2019 . At the festival , which runs from July 25 to Aug. 8 , there will also be concerts featuring young rising stars , collaborations between classical and world-music artists and an exploration of the Queer Songbook. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Roman Borys , Chamberfest&amp;rsquo ; s artistic and executive director , said presenting Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; essential&amp;rdquo ; chamber music would be the festival&amp;rsquo ; s mission not just this year , but in 2020 &amp;mdash ; 250 years after the great German composer&amp;rsquo ; s actual birth year &amp;mdash ; and in 2021. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s very nice at this point to be able to have a three-year Beethoven anniversary to sink our teeth into as we head into the next block of 25 years , &amp;rdquo ; Borys said in an interview . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;During the next three years , Chamberfest will feature Beethoven 's complete piano sonatas , violin sonatas , cello sonatas , piano trios and string quartets , as well as some other " smaller , idiosyncratic works , " Borys continued . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Beethoven is really sort of the centre of the world as far as chamber music is concerned ... ( responsible for ) the massive tome of art that really represents greatest achievement in Western culture , " Borys said . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43965;TOOLONG ... title= " Pianist Hinrich Alpers will perform 15 of Beethoven 's piano sonatas at this year 's Chamberfest. " alt= " Pianist Hinrich Alpers will perform 15 of Beethoven 's piano sonatas at this year 's Chamberfest. " data-portal-copyright= " HANDOUT " **28;44001;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 283620 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; This year&amp;rsquo ; s festival will see Berlin-based pianist Hinrich Alpers perform 15 of Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s 32 piano sonatas over two &amp;ldquo ; marathon&amp;rdquo ; afternoon concerts at Dominion-Chalmers United Church , on July 30 and Aug. 1 . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Borys called Alpers , a frequent performer at Chamberfest , &amp;ldquo ; a musician of immense breadth and depth . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Audiences have really learned to appreciate the grace and absolute control and mastery and insightfulness and beauty with which he approaches his interpretations , &amp;rdquo ; Borys said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also in the Essential Beethoven series , violinist James Ehnes will tackle the composer&amp;rsquo ; s 10 violin sonatas at Dominion-Chalmers during concerts on July 31 and Aug. 2 , joined by pianist Andrew Armstrong . As well , author and radio host Robert Harris , who wrote What To Listen For In Beethoven , will give two talks on Beethoven during Chamberfest. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Regarding baroque singer and composer Barbara Strozzi , who was born in Italy 400 years ago this August , Borys said &amp;ldquo ; the musical world has yet to truly understand her greatness . &amp;rdquo ; Strozzi&amp;rsquo ; s music will be heard during a July 26 concert at Dominion-Chalmers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In connection with the 200th anniversary of her birth , Clara Schumann , the wife of German composer Robert Schumann and a formidable Romantic-era composer in her own right , will be celebrated at an afternoon performance on Aug. 7 at the National Gallery of Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;This year 's Chamberfest will also mark two half-century anniversaries . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;In 1969 , The Beatles released Abbey Road . That classic album that has been reimagined and re-orchestrated by Toronto 's Art of Time Ensemble , the group that closed Chamberfest two years ago with its like-minded exploration of Sgt. Pepper 's Lonely Hearts Club Band . This year , the group will again close Chamberfest , delving into Abbey Road on Aug. 8 at Dominion-Chalmers. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Toronto group&amp;rsquo ; s 2017 concert &amp;ldquo ; was such a huge hit I couldn&amp;rsquo ; t resist this opportunity , &amp;rdquo ; Borys said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;The festival will present the Canadian Brass , which marks its 50th anniversary this year , on Aug. 2 at Dominion-Chalmers . " They 've created an amazing brand that has always celebrated and absolutely championed the highest level of artistry , " Borys said . " They combine that virtuosity with a lighthearted approach ... They 've embraced their inner entertainer . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; String quartets will be well-represented at Chamberfest thanks to concerts by the St. Lawrence String Quartet ( Jul. 27 at 1 p.m. , National Gallery ) , Quatuor Danel ( July 30 at 7 p.m. , Dominion-Chalmers ) and the Dover Quartet ( Aug. 7 at 7 p.m. , Dominion-Chalmers ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among the rising stars at Chamberfest this year are the Ottawa-raised duo of cellist Bryan Cheng and pianist Silvie Cheng , who perform Aug. 3 at the National Gallery . &amp;nbsp ; Another fresh-faced group returning to Chamberfest is the Netherlands-based all-female Syr&amp;egrave ; ne Saxafoonkwartet . The group , which played in Ottawa in 2016 , will play July 29 at 1 p.m. an the National Gallery . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among the festival&amp;rsquo ; s Chamberfringe concerts at 10 p.m. nightly are an opening concert in Dominion-Chalmers by the St. Lawrence String Quartet and pianist Steven Prutsman , playing along with the Buster Keaton film College , a July 26 concert at the Ottawa Art Gallery ( OAG ) where guitarist Ben Verdery will play music from Bach to Jimi Hendrix . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also in the Chamberfringe series , Toronto-based Cuban jazz pianist Hilario Duran and his trio will join forces at La Nouvelle Sc&amp;egrave ; ne on King Edward Avenue with violinist Annalee Patipatanakoon and Borys , who will pick up his cello . On Aug. 5 , Toronto&amp;rsquo ; s Al Qahwa Ensemble will present Arabic folk at the OAG and the following night , on Aug. 6 , the Toronto chamber-pop ensemble Queer Songbook Orchestra will perform music ranging from Billy Strayhorn to Taylor Swift at the OAG. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Festival passes ranging in price from $170 to $439 along with selected single tickets go on sale Tuesday at 9 a.m. at chamberfest.com/tickets and at the Chamberfest box office ( 4 Florence St. , Suite 301 ) . Pass prices increase on May 1 . More information : chamberfest.com. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;44031;TOOLONG " **32;44058;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; twitter.com/peterhum &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;44092;TOOLONG ... time to rethink the role of the Privy Council clerk , expert says on Wernick 's resignation&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;44127;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicSnoop Dogg , The Killers , Logic coming to Bluesfest 's 25th anniversary editionhttp : **33;44244;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;44279;TOOLONG ... title= " Snoop Dogg. " alt= " Snoop Dogg. " data-portal-copyright= " Mike Coppola " **28;44315;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1361384 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hiphop legend Snoop Dogg , modern rockers The Killers , cult rapper Logic , Russian feminist punks Pussy Riot and hardcore heroes The Offspring are among the acts coming to Ottawa this summer to help RBC Bluesfest celebrate its 25th anniversary . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With Scottish synth-pop band CHVRCHES , DJ/producer KYGO and Canadian rockers Alexisonfire also on the bill , it&amp;rsquo ; s another wildly diverse lineup for Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s biggest summer music festival , which returns to the grounds of the Canadian War Museum at LeBreton Flats Park from July 4-14 with more than 100 acts performing on four stages . But this year , for the first time , organizers are making it easier for fans to navigate the schedule by programming genre-specific blocks of music on the various stages . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Snoop Dogg , for example , will headline a rap night on July 13 that also features a return appearance by hip-hop pioneers Wu-Tang Clan , while Nashville singer Kane Brown shares the July 11 bill with the country-pop outfit Gone West . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44345;TOOLONG ... title= " The Killers . " alt= " The Killers . " data-portal-copyright= " Kevin Winter " **28;44381;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1361387 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In the past the vision was just to have as many different things on the same night to give everybody something , &amp;rdquo ; explained Bluesfest&amp;rsquo ; s artistic and executive director Mark Monahan in an interview . &amp;ldquo ; But we&amp;rsquo ; ve done it more by stage this year so you don&amp;rsquo ; t get a completely different audience from one act to another . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The festival starts July 4 with CHVRCHES in the headlining slot on the main stage , with support from the Swedish folk duo First Aid Kit , British R&amp;amp ; B singer Nao and electro-pop producer U.S. Girls . Also performing that day are folk singer-songwriter Steve Poltz and Little Steven and the Disciples of Soul . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Country superstar Eric Church plays July 5 on a previously announced program that includes Jason Isbell and the 400 Unit and Saskatchewan&amp;rsquo ; s Colter Wall . On July 6 , Kingston rockers the Glorious Sons graduate to headline status on a Saturday that also features U.S. rockers Taking Back Sunday and Canadian punks PUP. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; July 7 brings The Killers to the main stage , along with performances by the British post-punk crew Psychedelic Furs and the English &amp;lsquo ; 90s band James , as well as Indigenous singer-songwriters William Prince and Elisapie. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44411;TOOLONG ... title= " Chvrches ' Lauren Mayberry " alt= " Chvrches ' Lauren Mayberry " data-portal-copyright= " THC/ZJF " **28;44447;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1361385 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; After a day off on July 8 , the festival resumes on July 9 with a mixed bag of acts , including mainstage headliner KYGO , blues legend Buddy Guy , Nashville&amp;rsquo ; s Lennon Stella and Ottawa-born blues guitarist Sue Foley . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Logic takes the main stage on July 10 , with Jessie Reyez , Elle King , The Sheepdogs , Ziggy Alberts and The Devil Makes Three also on the bill . The aforementioned night with Kane Brown on July 11 also features appearances by Shakey Graves , Murda Beatz , DJ Pauly D , Leikeli47 and more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The festival&amp;rsquo ; s final weekend will be hopping , starting with a rock night on July 12 that features Alexisonfire , The Offspring and The Dirty Nil , as well as rapper T-Pain , the enigmatic Guided By Voices and more.Snoop Dogg headlines what&amp;rsquo ; s expected to be a hiphop celebration of cannabis legalization on July 13 , although Pussy Riot , the Russian feminist punk band , roots gentleman Pokey LaFarge , and troubadour Matt Andersen and the Mellotones are also scheduled for other stages that day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , it&amp;rsquo ; s the long-awaited return of the pop superstars Backstreet Boys on July 14 . They will share the day with blues legend Sugaray Rayford , soft-rockers America , pop singer Francesco Yates and more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The schedule also includes a selection of Ottawa-area acts , including Scary Bear Soundtrack , Silent Winters , Mia Kelly , Area Resident and Brea Lawrenson , to name a few . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A limited number of festival passes are available in a one-day presale that runs from 10 a.m. to midnight Thursday . Regular sales , including single-day tickets , and three-day and five-day passes , start at 10 a.m . Friday . Go to ottawabluesfest.ca for details . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; lsaxberg@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;44477;TOOLONG ... unit targets youth vaping in third wave of anti-smoking program&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;44513;TOOLONG ...
