ArtsMusicEscapade is celebrating its 10th anniversary : Here 's what to expecthttp : **34;44630;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;44666;TOOLONG ... title= " Escapade Music Festival took over Lansdowne Park for a mostly young crowd of energetic EDM enthusiasts in Ottawa on Saturday , June 23 , 2018. " alt= " Escapade Music Festival took over Lansdowne Park for a mostly young crowd of energetic EDM enthusiasts in Ottawa on Saturday , June 23 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " David Kawai " **28;44702;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1226718 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Escapade Music Festival&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; June 22-23 , Lansdowne Park &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tickets and info : escapademf.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Veteran New York DJ Danny Tenaglia will team up with Canadian techno legend Tiga to help Ottawa 's Escapade Music Festival celebrate its 10th anniversary this summer . The electronic dance music festival takes place June 22 and 23 at Lansdowne Park . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The two seasoned DJs , who will perform separate sets and one together , are the final acts to be announced in a lineup that already includes headliners DJ Snake and Holland 's Martin Garrix , along with Canadians Adventure Club , Zeds Dead , Keys and Krates , Loud Luxury and dozens of others from North America and beyond . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;For Paris-based Snake , whose real name is William Sami ? tienne Grigahcine , it will be his first appearance in Ottawa . The Grammy-nominated producer/artist is one of the world 's most popular crossover DJs , having hit the billion mark on Spotify with more than one song . His latest massive hit , Taki Taki , features Cardi B and Selena Gomez . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Garrix is another DJ with commercial appeal , ranked by DJ Mag as No. 1 on the list of the world 's top 100 DJs for three consecutive years . He first played Escapade in 2016. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44732;TOOLONG ... title= " Zeds Dead . " alt= " Zeds Dead . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;44768;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1359258 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The only female act on the bill at this time is &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Ruslana Malytska , the Ottawa-based DJ/producer better known by her stage name Khaos. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Bolstered by a partnership with concert-promotion giant Live Nation , the festival returns to Lansdowne Park for the third year , with two outdoor stages on the grounds and a stage inside the Aberdeen Pavilion . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The historic building , also known as the Cattle Castle , will be transformed with lighting and special effects , said Ali Shafaee , who 's part of the festival 's organizing team . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " It was a huge hit last year , " Shafaee said . " The first year we did n't put much emphasis on it in terms of production but this year we 'll be stepping up our game . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Although Aberdeen is also notoriously challenging for live sound , organizers found curtains softened the reverb last year . According to Shafaee , the style of music makes a difference , too . " It 's not like rock or country because there are not as many vocals or live instruments so the sound is n't as bad in there , " he said . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44798;TOOLONG ... title= " Martin Garrix performs onstage during day 4 of the Firefly Music Festival . " alt= " Martin Garrix performs onstage during day 4 of the Firefly Music Festival . " data-portal-copyright= " Michael Loccisano " **28;44834;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1359630 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Escapade began in 2010 as a Canada Day party in a ByWard Market parking lot . With Deadmau5 headlining , it attracted an estimated 2,000 people . In subsequent years , it was also held at the Ottawa Baseball Stadium , the RA Centre and Rideau Carleton Raceway before moving to Lansdowne Park in 2017. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Noise complaints and overdoses have been the biggest problems in recent years , although Shafaee said organizers work with the community and authorities to mitigate both . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " We 've had noise complaints but we have n't broken any bylaws or anything like that , " he said . " We 're a large event in an urban , residential neighbourhood . We have a sound system that allows us to control as much of the sound as we can , and we try to work with the community as best as we can . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;They also work with police and paramedics to ensure the safety of festivalgoers . More than 100 naloxone-equipped security personnel are on site , as well as first-aid trained Canadian Ski Patrol volunteers . An amnesty bin at the front gate allows patrons to dispose of street drugs , no questions asked . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44864;TOOLONG ... title= " The two-day Heineken Escapade 2017 electronic music festival has taken over Lansdowne Park . " alt= " The two-day Heineken Escapade 2017 electronic music festival has taken over Lansdowne Park . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;44900;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1027486 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " There 's no other festival in the region that does half the things we do , " Shafaee said . " We do whatever we can to make sure the audience is partying safely . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;This year 's event is expected to attract 30,000 people , most representing the 19-29-year-old demographic . Weekend passes are already sold out , and single-day passes are on track to sell out in the next six to eight weeks . If they go as quickly as anticipated , it will be the first time the festival has sold out in advance . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Shafaee said statistics show that up to 40 per cent of festivalgoers visit Ottawa from out of town , and the event generates an economic impact estimated at $3.1 million . Most visitors come from the Montreal and Toronto areas , plus a significant number from northern Ontario and a growing number from the United States . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44930;TOOLONG ... title= " Escapade Music Festival took over Lansdowne Park for a mostly young crowd of energetic EDM enthusiasts in Ottawa on Saturday , June 23 , 2018. " alt= " Escapade Music Festival took over Lansdowne Park for a mostly young crowd of energetic EDM enthusiasts in Ottawa on Saturday , June 23 , 2018. NewsMusicOttawa 's Kira Isabella back on track with new tuneshttp : **35;45737;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;45774;TOOLONG ... title= " Kira Isabella . " alt= " Kira Isabella . " data-portal-copyright= " Patrick Doyle " **28;45810;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1342239 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Aaron Pritchett&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;with guests Kira Isabella and David James&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;7:30 p.m . Feb. 14 , Algonquin Commons Theatre , 1385 Woodroffe Ave . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets : Win-to-get-in . Go to &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //iheartradio.ca " &gt;iheartradio.c ... &lt;strong&gt; for details . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45840;TOOLONG ... title= " Aaron Pritchett. " alt= " Aaron Pritchett. " data-portal-copyright= " Justin Tang " **28;45876;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1343380 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Much has changed in Kira Isabella 's life in the past few years . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;On a personal level , the Canadian country singer experienced some difficult moments , including a breakup and the loss of loved ones . And , even after three Top 10 singles and a Canadian Country Music Award , her career path was disrupted , too , by a shift in management and the end of her major-label deal . What 's more , she moved residences . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Happily , the Ottawa-born recording artist is back on track , with a new management team and a new batch of songs , recorded in Nashville , that show a fresh edge and definite maturity to her style of country music . Gone is the sweet-voiced teenager in a frilly dress ; the Kira Isabella we see now -- at 25 -- is a powerhouse vocalist with the confident air of a pop diva. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;She 's currently heading across the Prairies as a special guest of Canadian country cowboy Aaron Pritchett . Their Out On The Town excursion pulls into Ottawa on Valentine 's Day , the same day that Isabella plans to release an upbeat new song of longing entitled We Should Be Together . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;It 's the third single from her forthcoming third album , Sides , which has been released in two installments : Side A came out last fall , while Side B dropped earlier this month . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45906;TOOLONG ... title= " Kira Isabella . " alt= " Kira Isabella . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;45942;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1343378 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : The pop-oriented sound of these new songs is a bit of a departure for you . Are you striving for crossover appeal ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Well , never say never . That would be a really cool thing , although my heart is always in country . I grew up listening to a lot of pop music , and performing a lot of pop music and loving it . I always throw back to the Shania Twain album Up ! , where she had the country side and the pop side . They 're produced differently and that really resonated with me , and I think it comes out in the songs I write and the songs that I choose . There 's definitely an evolution happening in country music right now . So yeah , my producer and I wanted to kind of modernize it . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Speaking of Shania , you were one of the singers who performed with her during the televised opening of the Canadian Country Music Awards . What was that like ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;A : &; ... It was such an absolute honour . Shania was a big part of putting the performance together . She was at rehearsal and really took her time with us . It was so special because she 's such a big part of my story . She 's a big part of why I fell in love with country music , with her being from Timmins and me being from Ottawa . She 's an Ontario girl on a world stage . To get to do that with my friends , Madeline ( Merlo ) and &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Jess ( Moskaluke ) &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; it was really special . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Did anything surprise you about working with Shania ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;A : &; ... Not really . She was just what I expected . So sweet . She was really kind , and really took her time . And she smelled amazing . I 'll never forget that . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : The first single from this album was Little Girl , which talks about refusing to be treated like a little girl . Is that something you 've experienced ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;A : &; ... It 's definitely something that has happened to me a lot in my life . It happens when you work in this industry , especially when people are a lot older than you . It 's about making you feel small . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : It 's your second song with a strong feminist message . The first one , Quarterback , was about date rape . Is it important to you to be a role model ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;A : &; ... When I heard this song , I knew I had to do it . It was the same feeling I had with Quarterback . It resonated with me so much , and I knew I would be able to tell the story . It 's scary to think of being a role model because I 'm still growing up and learning . But I 've been through a lot in the last few years , and I 've realized the platform I have , and just the amount of people I 'm able to reach , you have to be careful what messages you send out . I would n't want to say anything that I would n't want any little girl to hear . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45972;TOOLONG ... title= " Kira Isabella . " alt= " Kira Isabella . " data-portal-copyright= " Patrick Doyle " **28;46008;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1342239 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : You released the new album in two parts . Are you releasing it as a full-length CD , too ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;A : &; ... I am . I think it 's so cool the way that artists are able to release music now . There is n't a formula or rules about how you have to put things out and do things anymore . There are artists who literally write a song and post it that day , which I think is so organic and so cool . As a fan of music , I love that . As an artist , I had half the album done and since it 's been a minute since I put out a body of work , my manager and I decided to spread it out and not make people wait for it . We always planned to put it out as a whole , too . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How did you divide the songs ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;&lt;strong&gt;A : &; ... Side A is the songs that have a little more attitude , that are a little bigger . Side B has more vulnerable songs . When we release them all together , it will be Sides . Being on a record label since I was 16 , I feel like the image that was put out for me , it was who I am at the time , but now I 'm a little bit older , and I want to be really honest with people and share my experiences , all the sides of me. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;46038;TOOLONG " **36;46069;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;46107;TOOLONG ... out best bets , Jan. 31 to Feb. 6&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;46142;TOOLONG ... ArtsMusicPatrick Watson teams up with NAC Orchestra to show emotional range of songwritinghttp : **29;46259;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;46290;TOOLONG ... title= " Montreal musician Patrick Watson . " alt= " Montreal musician Patrick Watson . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;46326;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1337969 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Patrick Watson with the NAC Orchestra&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Reviewed Saturday&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Patrick Watson and his band performed with the NAC Orchestra on Saturday , and the results were downright enthralling . Hunched over the grand piano and warbling in his expressive falsetto , the classically trained Montreal singer-songwriter was in his element amid the lush textures of Ottawa 's world-class orchestra . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;On a bitterly cold and snowy night in the capital , Watson and the dozens of musicians on stage -- conducted by Kingston Symphony music director Evan Mitchell -- created a sense of warmth and intimacy in front of a soldout crowd of more than 2,000 people at Southam Hall . The concert was part of the NAC Presents ' Sessions series , an initiative that matches singer-songwriters with the orchestra , and provides the artist with the opportunity to work with a pro to come up with arrangements of their material . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46356;TOOLONG ... title= " Montreal musician Patrick Watson . " alt= " Montreal musician Patrick Watson . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;46392;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1337969 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The unusual arrangements were definitely a highlight of the concert . While Watson 's songs are meticulously composed and layered to start with , the orchestral arrangements created by English conductor/arranger Jules Buckley , who 's 39 and greatly inspired by Looney Tunes composer Carl Stalling , brought them to another level with unexpected sounds and flourishes , including sliding whistles , squealing balloons and other non-traditional effects . Those surprises were used most effectively in the simmering build of Where The Wild Things Are . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The evening offered a superb demonstration of the emotional range of songwriting , from the hushed dreaminess of Lighthouse to the bustling peaks and lonely valleys of Beijing , to the soothing sentiment of To Build A Home , with its central lyric , " This is a place where I do n't feel alone . " Adding to the ethereal atmosphere were the mood-enhancing spotlights and a murky haze that emanated from a fog machine . There was also a chance for the orchestra to strut its stuff on a piece by Ravel. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;To Build A Home is one of Watson 's biggest hits , a quiet , piano-driven ballad that 's been streamed tens of millions of times , racking up numbers that rival the latest Lady Gaga mega hit . In an interview before the concert , Watson said its popularity has made him realize that people prefer to listen to something other than frenzied pop music when they get home . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46422;TOOLONG ... title= " Montreal musician Patrick Watson took a moment after rehearsals at the National Arts Centre on Saturday . " alt= " Montreal musician Patrick Watson took a moment after rehearsals at the National Arts Centre on Saturday . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;46458;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1337968 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " It does n't seem that people want to listen to crazy new rock ' n ' roll or catchy bubblegum pop music as much as they want to talk about it , " he said . " I think they want something that slows down time , and brings you out of this world . That 's what I 'm banking on . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Watson has been working on a new album , his first since 2015 's acclaimed Love Songs For Robots , which never got much radio play but helped him continue building an audience on both sides of the Atlantic . Last month 's European tour featured a string of soldout concerts in increasingly larger venues . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " There 's never any hype about what we do , " observed Watson . " We put out a record , and then slowly it digs into people 's lives . They play it more than once , and they show their friends . It just seems like the kind of music we do just slowly grows every day . And it 's a very young audience , which is interesting . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;It seems millennials are discovering Watson 's songs through other avenues , including TV shows , advertising and video games . Popular shows such as The Walking Dead , Homeland , This is Us and Orange is the New Black have all featured his work . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;The 39-year-old father of three young children said his forthcoming album may be his last full-length project , largely because of the music industry 's Spotify-driven shift to singles . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " It 's a different musical landscape , " he said . " I will probably never make another full-length record again . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;Whether or not it 's the last one , he 's determined to make it count . " I 've taken my time over the last four years , " he said . " I want to put out nine heavy-hitting songs . No fluff . To enter the Spotify world , which is so overwhelmingly flushed with so much music , I just did n't feel like putting anything out unless I felt it was really , really good . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " Instead of making a long 45-minute story , I 'm more interested in making songs that can stand on their own as well , outside the story , which is incredibly difficult . That 's why it 's taking so goddamn long . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt;He 's released two new songs so far , introducing the evocative Venezuelan-inspired Melody Noir during Saturday 's concert . There will be a third single soon , but we 'll have to wait until September for the full album . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-weight:400 ; " &gt; " I do n't want to flood the market , " he said . " Some people want to release a million things and maybe get some on a playlist . That works , but I just want to put something out that blows people 's minds . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;46488;TOOLONG " **36;46519;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;46557;TOOLONG ... battle blaze at Woodroffe Avenue home&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;46593;TOOLONG ... cold , howling wind and more snow for Sunday&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;46628;TOOLONG ... loss ' : Suspicious fire destroys historic wooden bridge in Low , Quebec&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Lynn SaxbergMon , 21 Jan 2019 13:15:13 **45;46663;TOOLONG ...
